  - **Two rules more, decided by the project lead on 23 November 2026** (on the measures above, the coordinator's two recommendations): (1) **carrying an infant slows its carrier**: one carrying an infant walks and gathers at `ages.carrying_pace_percent` (75 %) of its pace (`content/needs.ron`, D30), a gesture of the body in `World::grow`, never a rule "the mothers stay"; its far places weigh less at the fire as for any slow walker (deviation 2); (2) **look behind**: on its way, the caller who **sees** a member not yet there **walking** behind it, farther than `decisions.behind_tiles` (24) and within its sight, stops where it stands and waits, `behind_wait_minutes` (30) a day at most, looking again every `GROUP_WAIT_SECONDS`; it cannot wait for one it does not see, nor for one standing still (drinking, eating). The vivarium counts the members who stood at the place within `TOGETHER_SECONDS` (60) of their caller (`together`) and the callers' waits. **Measured, the five-year try, one seed, each rule alone then both** (PR 1 / carrying alone / looking behind alone / both): alive at the year's end 59 / 60 / 59 / 61; gathered 112.3 / 112.0 / 109.1 / 110.4 million kJ; carried at noon 1,625 / 1,689 / 1,188 / 1,279 thousand kJ; groups 648 / 655 / 473 / 702, ending home 408 / 372 / 206 / 308 and bare 225 / 267 / 265 / 383; those who joined walking behind their caller 30 / 27 / 27 / 25 %; the stretch 181 / 225 / 158 / 174 s on average over 132 / 114 / 62 / 95 groups; **the mothers of infants 152 / 182 / 132 / 198 tiles from the camp at noon against 146 / 149 / 117 / 129 for the other adults**. Read on one seed: the slower pace does **not** bring the mothers nearer: they go as far (their choice of a place by day, D53, does not weigh the pace) and stay out longer; looking behind shortens the stretch but its waits cost the groups their day (fewer come home full, the food carried at noon falls by a quarter). The ten-seed series says whether it holds.
  - **A fact of before, read in the groups**: about 15 % of the walks to a place stop short of it (the way not found: D31, D54), 11 % of the callers' with the decisions on; a group whose caller stops short ends `bare` before it is there.
  - **Measured on ten seeds** (the lab's `etalon-dix-graines` plan: two tribes of 25, twenty years, seeds 1 to 10, with and without trials, against the lot 5a series of the same day, merged as main a706d0b). **Without trials, better**: alive at twenty years 82.6 → 88.5 (6 seeds of 10 higher; seed 6, where a tribe fell, 43 → 86), births 75.8 → 79.7 (8 of 10), deaths of want 6.3 → 2.7, food carried at noon 1,574 → 1,893 thousand kJ (+20 %, 10 of 10), gathered 605 → 630 million kJ. **With trials, slightly worse**: alive 79.9 → 77.5 (8 of 10 lower), births 69.4 → 66.6 (7 of 10), deaths unchanged (39.5 → 39.1), carried at noon 2,663 → 2,824 (4 higher, 6 lower), trials kept 96 → 82.5. The lost births go with sleeping out: the share of minds sleeping out rose from 27 % to 33 %, and over the ten seeds its change and the change of births correlate at −0.70 (the conception asks for the spouse within reach at the season's dawn, D49); supposed here, shown on lot 5b's PR 1 bis control (sleeping out cut: births +10 %), whose conception over the season's first dawns answers it. A run is 17 % faster (1,205 → 1,004 s). A control with the decisions off (`--no-decisions`, seed 1) is not the reference itself: the infant carried slows its carrier there too.
  - **Deterministic** (D24): speakers by will then id, choices by worth then place, the look out by `scatter::roll` (`FIRE_LOOK`), no clock. The pages change (a rule of behaviour, outside D51's invariant). **Nothing stored** (D28 unchanged): the plans, groups and habits live in the vivarium's minds alone (D50). `Intention::Follow` is the simulation's own: **no protocol change** (no client asks it). No dependency; `plan/` untouched.
  - **Amended on 23 November 2026 (lot 5b, PR 1 bis) — a confidence by activity; the vocations, downstream, never assigned.** The project lead: "on ne veut jamais définir un métier tel quel… que les PNJ en viennent à se définir eux-mêmes… pas en amont, mais en aval, par conséquence". Each mind holds **a confidence by activity** of the content's list (`temperance.activities`, D30: today `explore`, `gather`, `stay`; later hunting, crafting), each moved by **the will's four rules** (D50, amended the same day: failures borne by innate will, a late success weighing more, the comeback to the innate will each season, being joined at the fire raising its gathering), each with its own streak, each starting at the innate will. **A day is judged from the world, objectively**, at nightfall (`News::Judged`): a look out by whether it found a place and told it new at the next dawn's fire (judged the next nightfall, the fire being read at dawn); a walk to a place (alone, called, joined) by whether it gathered a day of its need; a silence at the fire by whether none of its children under `follows_mother_years` was hungry at night (one without such a child is not judged). Each activity's success rate is a placeholder read on the five-year try of PR 1 bis (explore 75 %, gather 50 %, stay 90 %), so that average luck keeps a confidence where it is and a confidence says one's luck against the others'. **The choice at the fire leans** toward the more confident: the chance to go looking is weighed by its confidence in exploring against its confidence in gathering, ((explore + 5) / (gather + 5), between `habit_floor` % and three times): **a weight, never a ban**; it replaces PR 1's weight of the habit's look outs, which the habit now feeds through the judged days (one structure, the byte of PR 1 unchanged). Staying has no choice to lean yet: it gets one with the common heap (PR 3). **A vocation is only a word in the lab**: the activity of the highest confidence, over the years. The vivarium writes each one's will and confidences at each year's end, its days judged a success or a failure by activity, and the bold of each tribe-year (`docs/10`). **Decided alone, told**: the rarities read on the try rather than guessed (explore was first set at 30 %: its confidence rose for everyone); stay judged by the children only; the look out judged a day late; the lean as a ratio to gathering. Measured, the same twenty years: days judged explore 826 successes / 403 failures (67 %, under its 75 %: the confidence in exploring drifts down for most, and the look outs fall from 1,571 to 1,232), gather 11,656 / 11,631 (50 %), stay 5,621 / 421 (93 %); the vocation at the end, the activity of the highest confidence, gathering for 51, staying for 31, exploring for none. The five-year try (two tribes of 25, 250 days, seed 1, against PR 1): alive 59 / 59 at the year's end, gathered 109.2 / 112.3 million kJ, looked 304 / 568 days, the bold 4 / 1 at the end.
  - **Amended on 23 November 2026 (lot 5b, PR 1 bis, after its causes) — the habit leads, the confidence weighs; a success is worth its odds.** The causes, each proved by a control of one change (`content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/LOT5B-1bis-causes.md`): a day's success gained what keeps the average flat, so almost nothing where successes are common (0.02 point for a look out that found, none for a quiet night); the confidence in exploring moved only by bad luck and the comeback to the innate will, and the lean to look stayed the innate one (tribe A looked 41 % less, tribe B 67 % more, on four seeds of four); the rarity itself changed nothing (75 or 67 %: the same run). The project lead: "je suis d'accord avec tout". (1) **The chance to go looking is again the habit's weight of PR 1** (each look out that found adds `habit_weight`, each one for nothing takes it away), **times** the confidence ratio above (between `habit_floor` % and three times), never under `habit_floor`: it replaces the ratio alone. (2) **A day that succeeds gains one failure's cost times the odds against it**, `failure_milli` × (100 − rarity) / rarity (`Temperance::day_success_milli`: a third of a point for a place found, one for a day fed, a ninth for a quiet night); failures cost as before; the will keeps its own gain (`success_milli`). Average luck now raises a confidence a little (the failures borne free cost nothing); the season's comeback to the innate will holds it.
  - **Measured, PR 1 bis whole and change by change** (the standard plan's runs, two tribes of 25, twenty years, seeds 1 to 4, against #327 on the same seeds; each change added alone on the one before, its own control): alive at twenty years #327 77.8; the will tempered alone 73.2; with the habit leading and the season's first dawns 79.2 (births 68.2 → 78.8, food carried at noon 2,434 → 3,180 thousand kJ, 4 of 4, but one seed's famine: 35 dead of hunger); with a success worth its odds 84.5 (deaths of want 10.0 → 1.8); with the slow ways on their horizon **90.8** (4 of 4 above #327: births 81.5, carried at noon 3,442, gathered 623 → 675 million kJ, days looking 1,620 → 3,048, deaths of want 0.2). Without trials, the same build lives better too (#327 87.0 → 93.5 alive, 4 of 4): the dawns help every wife. **The price of trying** (with trials less without, the same four seeds) falls from −9.3 alive and −9.0 births (#327) to **−2.8 and −4.3** on those four seeds. **On the ten seeds** (the standard plan, with and without trials, against #327's series): alive 77.5 → **88.4** with trials and 88.5 → **96.8** without (10 of 10 both), births 66.6 → 80.0 and 79.7 → 88.2 (10 of 10), gathered 620 → 664 (9 of 10) and 629 → 671 million kJ (10 of 10), days looking 1,537 → 2,878 with trials (10 of 10) but 3,498 → 2,669 without (10 of 10 lower: without trials, a day fed weighs three times a place found); food carried at noon 2,824 → 2,860 with trials (6 of 10: the four seeds' +41 % was their luck) and 1,893 → 1,989 without (10 of 10); deaths 39.1 → 41.6, of illness, with more alive. The price of trying on ten seeds: −11.0 → **−8.4** alive, −13.1 → −8.2 births. A run is 23 % longer (more people). The horizon in days is a season of the vivarium's fifty-day year; at 13 days the four seeds gave 81.5 alive against 90.8, a gap with no mechanism found, read as the four seeds' noise until measured on ten.
  - **Amended on 23 November 2026 (lot 5b, PR 1 ter) — who is believed to know, the questions, the evening's review.** The project lead: "on peut mettre ça en place... les améliorer et les pousser un peu plus loin". Three gestures at the fire, each a belief or a dated fact, never a level or a title (D30): (1) **reputation** (`content/needs.ron`, section `reputation`): each body holds, for each of `activities` (explore, gather), the one it believes best there with its own credit, raised by a day it followed one and came back fed (`seen_gain` 20) or an account heard (`heard_gain` 10), worn by a day it came back empty (`wear` 15), fading a point a day; it joins first the call of whom it believes best at gathering (`join_percent`); the belief is a `Record::Reputed` of the common memory (D55); (2) **the questions**: one with no place to go asks whom it believes best "where is food?" and gets that one's best place, `questions_per_night` (4) a fire, the success of the answer counting as the answerer's; (3) **the evening's review** (section `decisions`): a caller keeps `facts_kept` (32) dated facts on its companions ("I waited for 14 on the 12th"), judges each on its last `review_outings` (3) outings, sets aside for tomorrow one with `review_bad` (2) bad ones (a failure of its confidence in gathering) and calls first by name one with two good ones; a parent never sets aside its own child; one set aside weighs first the places within `near_camp_tiles` (40) of the camp. `decisions.review_parts` (set_aside, aside_costs, names) lets a run hold to part of it, all by default. A day's success after successes in a row weighs more (`temperance.streak_percent`, up to twice after four). **Measured on ten seeds against #329** (two tribes of 25, twenty years; `content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/compte-rendu-1ter-causes-2026-11-23.html`): alive 88.4 → 88.7 with trials, 96.8 → 98.3 without; deaths −1.9 with trials (9 of 10); the review whole is the only version without a collapse on ten seeds, its parts alone are not (the review decomposed, same folder). The vivarium's JSON gains `bilan` and `seasons` (docs/10).
  - **The plan for PR 2, amended on 24 November 2026 — no vote, no group rule: each listener decides for itself.** The project lead replaced "a majority moves the whole camp": a trusted person comes to the camp's fire and tells of a better place; each listener (a family, a lone man, a lone woman) decides for itself, by its trust in the teller (reputation), its own lived grade of where it lives (D53, the notebook) and its company. "Sur les 20 personnes qui l'écoutent, il y en a 15 qui sont d'accord et qui y vont. Mais ils n'ont pas choisi comme groupe, c'est plusieurs individualités." **Company becomes a sense of the notebook** (one more `brain::Sense`, D53): how many it lived among at a place enters the place's grade by itself, so a place the others went to draws by what the body lives, not by a count of votes; and some go and check the place before the others follow (testers, a trial judged as any other, D50). Nothing of it is built yet. Today (code reading, 24 November 2026), the fire's circle is keyed on each body's exact home at dusk: those who moved never hear nor tell at the camp's fire, only at their new home's, and only when two or more lie there.
  - **Also for PR 2, with the scouts (24 November 2026): telling the way.** One who found the way past water or rock tells it at the fire, the ford as a place, and a listener walks via it in two legs; until then a mark of seen, not reached (D53) clears only under the body's own feet.
  - **Amended on 24 November 2026 (lot 5b, PR 2 b) — the call to move, each one's balance, the held goal.** Built as the project lead decided it (no vote; the call is the call/join gesture applied to the home; the caller goes even alone; one home at a time). **The call**: at most one a fire an evening, by the most willing present holding a home its notebook gave (`Act::Moves`; D50). **Each listener present weighs it at its own balance** (the call is the evening's first word: see the couples below): the gain told × its trust in the caller / 100, plus the company it would lack here less the company it would lack there, by its own sociability (D53); here, the present who do not go; there, those who already said they go, the caller first, with their children. Above a hundredth of a day (the margin of `lived_home`) it joins (`Act::Joined`, its gathering decision given up for the day); otherwise it stays. Its trust is its credit in the caller at a new reputation activity, `live`, if it believes the caller best at it (moved by its own home trial's verdict: `seen_gain` kept, `wear` given up; never asked "where is food?"), else the credit of a teller (D55). A child under `follows_mother_years` follows its mother as ever; one under sixteen or under a trial does not weigh it. Who joins walks behind the caller as a group (`Group.moves`), with the wait at dawn and the look behind; the group ends at the new home, where each lives its day. **The couples (the project lead's decision of 24 November 2026)**: the existing couples stay (D49), because modelling looser early-human bonds would be complicated and would touch sensitive subjects; each spouse still decides alone at its own balance, but in its company term the spouse counts as `company.spouse_counts` companions (D53): a spouse who goes pulls the other, which can still stay. **The held goal** (`Mind.goal`, at most one): for now only walking to the new home when it lies farther than a day's walk (the body's pace on the reference ground through the hours of light). By day it comes before the day's decision; drinking and eating interrupt it, and at night the body lies down where it stands; then it goes on. The body says stop: half the load it set off with gone, hungry with nothing to eat on it, thirsty with no water it knows within a day; nothing counts the days. Stopped, the move is given up (D50). `GoalTo` is an enum so that telling at a camp and testing a place (PR 2 c) plug in. Choices made by the AI session, listed in the pull request: no maximum duration; no gathering on the way; the most willing caller first. **The call is the evening's first word (the project lead, 24 November 2026)**: in the plan the call came at its caller's turn in the order of will, and those who had spoken before it never heard it; in this PR's control run (seed 9, five years) 15 of 19 couples split that way, births 30 against 38 on main. The lead: a split for so trivial a cause, a mere order of speaking, goes against keeping couples together, and couples kept together, living at a better home, are what should raise births; so the call is said first and everyone present hears it, the others keeping their order. Reason: the plan of PR 2 validated by the project lead on 24 November 2026 (version "the call and the visit", this PR its part (b)); every rule a perception or a gesture: the caller's notes, the listener's trust and company, the body's own stop.
  - **Amended on 24 November 2026 (lot 5b, PR 2 b) — the spouse under a trial hears, the absent spouse waits.** The project lead, 24 November 2026: a couple decides together, so **a spouse under a trial hears the call** and weighs it like anyone; if it joins, its trial ends unjudged (no will changed, no credit moved) and it opens the move's home trial; others under a trial still do not weigh it. And "s'il n'est pas avec son conjoint pour faire la décision, il reporte la décision, en attendant la présence de son conjoint": **a listener whose living grown spouse is not at the fire keeps the call** (`Mind.kept_call`: the place, the gain told, the caller, those already going) without weighing it. At the first fire where both are present, before any other word, both weigh it, the holder trusting the first caller, the spouse trusting the holder, each counting the other as `company.spouse_counts` companions (D53); who joins opens its own home trial there, with the held goal if the place lies beyond a day; there is no group with the first caller. The kept call is dropped when the spouse dies or a newer call is heard. A spouse who hears and says no still stays. Choices made by the AI session, listed in the pull request: any living spouse lets a body under a trial hear; those going are counted when the call is kept; in a retold call the more willing of the two weighs first and the second, if it joins, walks behind the first; a dead spouse's kept call is dropped at the next fire. Measured (seed 9, five years): 9 calls, all in tribe B in its fifth year, 2 called and 7 retold; of 20 weighings 3 joined; 8 listeners kept a call for an absent spouse, and the 7 retold calls gave 1 couple moved together, 1 split (one joined, the other said no) and 5 stayed together. The caller's own spouse, absent from its fire, was not covered by the decision and stayed behind both times: left to the project lead.
  - **Amended on 24 November 2026 (lot 5b, PR 2 b) — the caller waits for its spouse, and weighs its own going after a no.** The project lead, 24 November 2026: the postponement was meant for the caller too. **A body whose living grown spouse is not at its fire does not call** that evening, nor goes alone at no fire: it keeps the call (`Mind.calling`, the same place and gain) and calls at the first evening fire where its spouse is present, where the spouse weighs it like any listener and can still say no. And **after its spouse says no, the caller weighs its own going** with the listeners' balance (`weigh_move`): the gain it sees there, trusting itself (100), the company it would lack here less there, its spouse counting `company.spouse_counts` and staying. Above the margin it goes alone, as before; otherwise it stays with its spouse: its trial is not opened, the call is kept for a later evening with its spouse present, and those who said "I come" that evening stay too, no trial opened (`News::MoveCalled.own`). Choice made by the AI session: while it holds a call, a body draws no other trial (otherwise a new trial would replace the call). Measured (seed 9, five years): one caller of tribe B, its spouse saying no each time, stayed and called again 243 evenings, from day 7 to the end, with a gain of 5 per mille: the kept call never ends, and while held no other trial starts. Left to the project lead.
  - **Amended on 24 November 2026 (lot 5b, PR 2 b) — a move must repay its cost; who stays forgets** (the project lead, 24 November 2026: "il faut vraiment que le déménagement apporte quelque chose": the bodies better their standard of living and move only to a place that is really better, bringing over the medium and long term more food, more children, more of everything). **A move's gain a day must pass the margin plus its cost spread over a horizon** (`brain::move_cost`): the walk from the body's own home to the place, its energy and its time valued as in the reach grade (D53), plus a day of the body's own gathering lost to settling, over `trials.move_repay_seasons` (one season, 12 days; a D30 placeholder). So a near place a little better is taken, a far place barely better is not. It applies wherever a move is decided: the caller's own choice (`lived_best`), each listener's balance (`weigh_move`: the gain told times its trust, less its own cost from its own home), and the caller's weighing after its spouse's no. No discount for a little-known place (an option for later). **The caller who stays forgets** (the project lead): once it decides to stay with its spouse, its call is dropped and its trial ended unjudged; a better place proposed later, its own or another's, is decided afresh, again as a couple. Measured (seed 9, five years): 83 alive, 0 deaths of want, 39 births; 8 calls; one move of tribe B on day 135 (+398 by the caller's notes, 17 joined, 4 couples split by a chosen no), all 20 home trials given up after their season with the new home graded lower than the old (871 to 1050 against 1035 to 1222); two callers of tribe A stayed for their spouse and forgot.
  - **Amended on 24 November 2026 — the tale makes others go and see, never move.** The synthesis (`content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/synthese-penseurs-demenager-2026-11-24.md`, points 4 and 5), validated by the project lead. A surprise lived is told at the fire where it is lived: each other grown present, but the teller's spouse (who took the day), with no notes of its own of that grove (a listener who lived there trusts its own notes) and who believes it (the surprise × its credit in the teller, D55, at the threshold or more), goes to see it today by the chance of its will (`moving.see_percent_by_will`, a fifth for the timid, four fifths for the bold; `FIRE_SEE` from the run's seed): at the fire it says it goes alone to the place where the teller gathered (`Act::Alone`, the existing gesture), the teller's record of the place heard if it held none (`News::GoSee`). Its own day there is then compared at the next fire like anyone's: only days lived make a candidate. **The departure** keeps all of PR 2 b (the call at the fire, each one deciding alone, the spouse counting, waiting for an absent spouse, the caller who stays and forgets, the cost repaid over the horizon), with two changes: the call is made only as D50 amended says, and **a listener who lived at the place called weighs it by its own notes** (`brain::own_gain`, trusting itself), not by the gain told. The caller's trial news (`News::Trying`) is now told when it sets off, no longer when it felt the move; the feeling is `News::MoveFelt`. Measured: on the three seeds, 412 to 648 surprises, 520 to 1,114 days gone to see, 3 to 12 calls (2 to 9 felt, 1 to 6 by a huge surprise alone), 25 to 28 % of the grown dawns pushed; 4 to 10 couples split at a call (one joined, the other said no), and couples apart at the dawns of conception rose (seed 9: 166 against 40; seed 2: 448 against 37), the measured cause of the births lost. Left to the project lead: whether a spouse who stays should follow later, and the push threshold.
  - **Amended on 24 November 2026 — the family weighs apart from company, more with children.** The project lead, 24 November 2026, after the long runs' partings (spouses whose homes parted after one went and the other said no): "un poids de la famille à part de la compagnie", added to it, "double effet sur une personne qui aime la compagnie et 0 sur une personne qui n'aime pas la compagnie, mais la famille a quand même un impact"; "s'il y a des enfants, il y a encore plus de poids". Why: the spouse's weight lived only in the company term, which a loner does not feel at all and which a crowd saturates (more than two to six others add nothing), so in a village a spouse who went pulled nobody. Now, when a body weighs a move called at the fire (`weigh_move`, the listeners and the caller weighing its own going after its spouse's no), each member of its family it would leave behind costs it, wherever that member stands, whatever its sociability and however many others are around: its spouse `family.spouse_permille` (250, a quarter of a day of need a day), each of its children too young to weigh a move (under sixteen) `family.child_permille` (100), on their mother's side (a child of its own body goes with it and weighs nothing either way). A member who goes pulls it there, one who stays holds it here. It pulls, never forces: a gain worth more still wins, and a no is still a no. The company term is unchanged (`company.spouse_counts` stays): a sociable body feels both. D30 placeholders in `content/needs.ron`, section `family`; absent, the family weighs only as company. Storage and protocol unchanged.
  - **Amended on 24 November 2026 — the call weighed in rounds, each knowing the others' answers.** The study of the camps that never move (branch `probe/camp-moves`): weighing one after the other by decreasing will, a spouse said no because the other had not spoken yet, and the other no because its spouse had said no; company and family held whole camps in place. The project lead's correction, 24 November 2026: a couple weighs with each other's answer known. When a call is made (`brain::weigh_in_rounds`), the listeners still to speak whose spouse is at the fire weigh it together in rounds: a hand goes up for each whose own worth of the move (the gain by its credit less the cost of the walk, kept as `Weighed::worth`) passes the margin; then, round after round, the hands are lowered of those whose balance, the raised hands known (the company left behind, a spouse going or staying, the family), no longer passes, until nothing changes; then raised, of those the raised hands now pull over the margin, until nothing changes. Each then says, at its turn, what the rounds gave it. A kept call (the absent spouse's, above) is weighed by the pair the same way. No new perception and no new number: the balance is the one above. Decided alone, told: the rounds start from the worth alone, not from every hand up (a first try did: every sociable camp moved on a gain of 5 per mille, a herd); lowering before raising; the vivarium tells each weighing's worth (`move_calls[].weighed[].worth_permille`). **Measured** (seed 1, 144 days, on top of the way home of D53 amended the same day): at richness 1.0 no call was made, before or after (the surprise had no yardstick: D53 amended below), so the runs are identical; at 7.3 births 99 → 100, couples together 91 → 94 %, couples' homes parted 11 → 0, calls 3 → 2, joined 4 → 8.
  - **Amended on 25 November 2026 — the move fed by the trips.** The places found on a trip become candidates for the call to move (a day ended at no fire judged against one's own usual days), and a camp whose surroundings the body knows bare weighs less in its own choice of a place (`lived_best`): D53, amended the same day, point (5). The call itself is unchanged. Bodies away on a trip are absent from the fire, so they say nothing there (as any absent).
  - **25 November 2026 (D09 amended)**: `habit_days` is written `habit_seasons`, 4: a day's byte for each day of a cycle (64 in calendar v1).
  - **Amended on 26 November 2026 — "near" in game minutes of walk.** The project lead, 24 November 2026: "Faut le passer non en mètres, mais en temps de marche"; the plan `content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/cerveau-temps-de-marche-plan-2026-11-24.md`, its seven decisions validated on 26 November 2026 as recommended (the plan's step 4; decision 5 keeps couples, courtship and orphans in tiles). One set aside at the fire weighs first the places within `decisions.near_camp_minutes` (3 game minutes of its walk, 48 tiles on open ground, 32 in the forest) of the camp, no longer within `near_camp_tiles` (40 on each axis). A day's best grove counts as near home, no news at the fire, within `GROVE_NEAR_MINUTES` (4 game minutes, 64 tiles on open ground, 43 in the forest), no longer within a grove's side on each axis. The same meaning on open ground; nearer in the forest. A straight walk, so a circle, no longer a square. Nothing stored, **no protocol change, no storage change, no dependency**.
  - **Amended on 26 November 2026 (the audit of wakes, step 3) — the look behind is a glance, not a thought.** The project lead, 26 November 2026, on the audit `content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/audit-reveils-2026-11-26.md` (§2.3 point 2): "All these useless thoughts are huge... modify it totally", and "we don't want robots": never remove a real reflection.
    - **What changes.** (1) The caller walking to the group's place still looks behind every `GROUP_WAIT_SECONDS` (4), by the same rule (a member it sees walking behind it, farther than `behind_tiles`, while it has waited less than `behind_wait_minutes`), but as a glance done without a thought, like the point of no return (D53): it thinks only when it sees one to wait for. Its full look (water, food in sight) is no longer taken every 4 seconds of its walk. (2) The caller waiting at dawn for its members is woken by them: when the last it waits for has woken and come within reach, or its hour of waiting is up, no longer every 4 seconds. (3) A member waiting by its caller thinks when its caller sets off (or its hour is up). The caller stopped for a straggler still thinks every 4 seconds (it waits on purpose); the member full waiting for its caller keeps its 10 minutes. Measured in the audit (seed 18): 10.9 thoughts a person-journée, 97 % changing nothing.
    - **Content** (D27, D30): `needs.ron`, section `decisions`, `behind_glance: true`; `false`, or absent, is the rule before (`--old-wakes`).
    - Nothing stored (a mind's glance lives in memory), **no protocol change, no storage change, no dependency**.
  - **Amended on 26 November 2026 (the audit of wakes, step 6) — children judged as seen.** The project lead, 26 November 2026: "No telepathy: a person knows what is at the camp when she sees it". The audit `content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/audit-reveils-2026-11-26.md`, §3.2 B.
    - **What changes.** At nightfall a parent who said nothing at the fire judges its day of staying (`stay`, the tempered will) by whether its children under `follows_mother_years` are fed (`kin_fed`): now only by those it sees, both at its camp (`at_camp`) at nightfall; a child away does not judge its day, and with none in sight the day is not judged. Measured in the audit (seed 18): 0.63 parent-child pairs read a person-journée, 61 % with the child out of sight.
    - **Content** (D27, D30): `needs.ron`, section `temperance`, `kin_seen: true`; `false`, or absent, is the rule before (`--old-wakes`).
    - Nothing stored, **no protocol change, no storage change, no dependency**. The other reads the audit lists (the call heard out of earshot, the children's home, the spouse alive: step 7) are not built.
- **Amended on 27 November 2026 — the absent noticed at the fire, and searched where last seen (the orphan infant).** The project lead, 27 November 2026: "people notice that someone is absent from the fire, and go and search the last place they knew her to be". Measured on a long run (seed 24, 960 days): five infants of 5 to 14 days died of thirst, each where its mother had died of illness 130 to 470 tiles from the camp while carrying it, exactly three days later; nobody went, because nobody knew, and that rule stays: no telepathy.
  - **What a mind keeps.** Of each close one (spouse, parent, child) it sat with at a dawn's fire: the day, where it stood, and the place it said there it was going to that day (its plan's place); seen again within its sight when it thinks, the day and the place follow. A close one whose home is elsewhere is known gone to live there. Nothing else: the true position of an absent is never read.
  - **Noticing.** At the dawn's fire, once the day is said, a close one not at this fire and not seen for `search.after_nights` nights (2: one night out is common, the project lead, 27 November 2026) is noticed absent. One kin present goes: its spouse first, then a parent, then a child; `search.from_years` old (16), holding no goal, not moving today. It goes to the place the absent said it was going to, else to the last place it saw it; when that is the camp itself, nobody goes. It leaves the group of its day (its own, called, ends as one whose caller left). One search an absence: the absence ends when the two sit at a fire again.
  - **Searching.** By day it walks there; the night, thirst and hunger interrupt the walk, as a held goal's. As it walks it sees, within its own sight only, the absent alive (the search ends, nothing more for now), its body lying dead or its infant lying alone, or, standing at the place, nothing (the search ends; stopped short of the place by water or rock, the same).
  - **The infant.** By the body it looks for an infant lying alone within its sight, walks to it, takes it up and carries it home, its choice of the day as one who turns back from a trip. The existing rules keep it alive: any grown woman who carries an infant nurses it (lot 3 B: milk while her reserve is not spent, the same rule that gives a dead mother's infant to the nearest woman), so a woman who finds it nurses it at once; a man, home, gives it to a grown woman he sees at the camp, one carrying none first, then the nearest. No rule of milk was added.
  - **One gesture of the simulation's own**: `Intention::Carry { body, infant, carrier }`, an infant nobody carries taken up within reach, or one's own burden given to a body within reach; like `Feed`, the brains' alone, no client asks it, refused without a word.
  - **No engineered feeling**: nothing weighs the search against the day; kin go because kin are missed. What the care for the young (D58) would weigh (who goes, how far) is not built.
  - **Decided alone, told** (the build): the amendment sits here rather than in D53 or D58, the absence being a thing of the fire; the order of kin; one searcher an absent; one search an absence; a searcher stopped short gives up; the hand-over to a woman at the camp; a corpse is known as its dead's by who sees it lying.
  - **Content** (D27, D30): `needs.ron`, section `search`; absent, nobody notices and nobody goes, bit for bit as before (`--no-search` in the vivarium).
  - **Measures** (`docs/10`, block `search`): absences noticed, searches begun, found alive, found dead, not found, infants taken, brought home, given to a woman, and what became of each a season later.
  - Nothing stored, **no protocol change, no storage change, no dependency**.
- **Amended on 27 November 2026 (the same day, after the first run read) — who is missed, and what is told.** The run of seed 33 (480 days, `after_nights` 2) gave 1,100 absences noticed and 757 searches for one infant taken: about 85 % for a kin of the same hearth out two nights or more, 104 pairs of kin searched 3 to 9 times, 96 found already at the searcher's own camp, 218 absences noticed by a child too young to go. The project lead approved five refinements; all of it is perceived or told, nothing read from the truth, every value in `needs.ron`, section `search`.
  - **"She often stays out long."** Of each close one a mind keeps the longest absence from the fire it came back from, in nights (`usual_max_nights`, 16 at most; forgotten `usual_seasons`, 4, after it was lived). An absence is noticed only past that, and never before `after_nights`.
  - **"She is at the camp."** A close one a present sees from the fire, within its sight, is seen: not absent, whatever fire it lay at.
  - **"She lives there."** A close one whose home is another hearth is known to live there: said at a shared fire, said to the searcher who finds it alive (met, it says where it lives), or told by who found it. For `lives_seasons` (4) its not sitting at one's fire is no absence; sitting there again with the same home ends it.
  - **Told at the fire.** A searcher keeps what it found (`findings_kept`, 4) and tells it at its next fire with someone to hear, `told_per_fire` (2) at most: found alive, there, living there; or found dead, there. Each present who holds that one as a close one takes it: alive, it is seen that day and that absence is searched; dead, it waits for it no more.
  - **A child tells.** Noticed by children alone (under `from_years`), the fire hears it. A grown one present who holds the absent as a close one and did not miss it says why: the children take what it knows (how long it stays out, where it lives) and nobody goes. With none, the grown one present who cares most for the young (the innate care of D58), free today, goes to the place the child says; it is no kin, and tells what it finds at the next fire like any searcher.
  - **Decided alone, told** (the build): the findings are told in the dawn's circle, where the absences are noticed, and kept on the mind, not written as a new kind of record in the well (D55): bounded by `told_per_fire` and `findings_kept`; the absent met alive says where it lives (a telling, at the searcher's sight); the longest absence is counted between two shared fires; for a child, the grown kin who knows comes before any helper, and the helper is chosen by its care, not drawn; a finding told is told once.
  - **Measures** (`docs/10`): `search.not_noticed` by cause, `search.heard_alive`, `search.searches_told_by_a_child`, the records' `noticed`, `not_noticed_because` and `told_by_child`, and `meta.timing.search_s`.
  - Nothing stored, **no protocol change, no storage change, no dependency**; `--no-search` unchanged.

### D57 — The hands: three gestures, two materials, no recipe; a container found or not
- **Reason**: the project lead, 24 November 2026, validating the first invention experiment (`content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/sac-invention-2026-11-24.md`) with "C'est parfait": two objects in the world, three gestures, no recipe, to see whether the people find alone how to carry more, keep it, remake it when it tears and pass it on, within a tribe and then to the other. D50 asked for it ("when fire, shelter or storage come, they come as materials with hidden properties, a few operations and objects defined by what they do"); the lead's knowledge model of 22 November 2026 says how it is held: no levels, a deed that worked is a record of the well (D55), knowing is holding the access, mastery is having practised the gesture in one's own body, and hearing does not make one able.
- **Consequences**:
  - **What the materials are, not what to make of them** (`content/materials.ron`, `content/species.ron`, D27): a material may be `sharp` (it cuts what one cuts with it in hand: flint), `folds_into` another material keeping its grams (birch bark into folded birch bark), hold loose food as a container (`holds_g`: folded birch bark, 4,000 g) and wear out (`wears_days`: 16, a season of the vivarium's year, then it tears and is gone, as food spoils). A tree species may have a `bark`, the material a cut takes from its trunk (the birch). The words "bag" or "box" appear nowhere in the code.
  - **Flint lies on the ground**, where it really lies: in the river gravels (grass within 3 tiles of water, and the ford) and on the rocky ground at the foot of the rock, 150 per thousand tiles, 80 under the trees by the rock (`content/spawns.ron`; the 10 November line "flint broken from the rock" is superseded for now by the lead's validation). **Amended on 25 November 2026** (the project lead on the first runs, where the people never found a flint and a birch together: "on peut en mettre beaucoup plus"): it was 20 per thousand on bank grass within 4 tiles and by the rock, 80 flints in the 768 valley; now 426 there. A nodule every six or seven tiles is what a gravel bar or a scree of flint-bearing chalk shows; the weight is a few hundred more items of the ground, sent as the pebbles are.
  - **Three gestures** (`sim::hands`): pick up (D35's act); **cut** a tree within gathering reach with a thing carried, taken in hand (`Intention::Cut`); **fold** a thing carried (`Intention::Fold`). A cut or a fold takes `hands.gesture_minutes` (10 game minutes) whatever comes of it; a walk, sleep or any other intention ends it with nothing. What comes of it is what the materials are: a cut with a sharp thing at a tree with a bark gives a piece of it on the body; a fold of a thing that folds turns it into what it folds into, made now; anything else gives nothing. The world tells `Event::Gestured` (what came of it) or `Event::GestureRefused`.
  - **Carrying** (D35 amended, below): the arms hold `movement.hands_g` (1 kg) of loose food; each container carried holds its `holds_g` more; whole objects (a branch, a log, a flint) count against the whole load (`carry_capacity_g`, 20 kg) alone, as before. Gathering stops and a pick-up of food is refused when the arms and containers are full.
  - **The hands are a way** (D50, `Way::Hands`, `Ways::hands`): the deeds a body knows, a deed being a gesture on a kind of thing (a material, or a tree's species for a cut) with a kind of thing in hand for a cut, six at most. A trial of the hands is drawn like the other ways, by the will (`wants_to_try`), only when the content has them (`hands.tries_per_day` above 0). On nothing heard, the body draws from the run's seed (salts `HANDS_GESTURE`, `HANDS_THING`) a gesture and a thing among those it carries and those lying within `hands.near_tiles` (4) or the trees standing there, at most `hands.tries_per_day` (4) a day, steps there and does it: no suggestion, any thing, any tool. A deed from which something came is learnt (added to its ways) and the trial's days count from that day, so that the judge sees the days with the new thing; a deed that gave nothing is not learnt. The judge is D50's; a trial of the hands in which nothing came of anything is not kept. Not kept, the deeds tried are forgotten and what they brought is let go, one thing a thought: a body carries only food and what its deeds use or give.
  - **The plain minimum, never a goal**: a body redoes the deeds it knows when it lacks what they give (nothing carried of what the deed gives, nor of what its folds turn that into) and finds the thing within its sight of the hands as its day passes; it never walks to look for a birch or a flint. So a container that tears is remade when a flint is carried and a birch stands near, or not.
  - **A gesture only with what allows it** (amended 25 November 2026, the project lead on the second runs, where every cut was made with food in hand: "on ne coupe pas avec une baie"). In a trial on nothing heard, a body cuts only with a sharp thing and only a tree with bark, and folds only what folds; it picks up anything. Seeing that a stone is sharp, that a sheet bends, that a trunk has bark, is a true perception of a material's property (`sharp`, `folds_into`, a species' `bark`), not a suggestion: the gesture stays drawn at random among those the things at hand allow, and nothing says which one leads anywhere. A flint picked up is not held for the trial's days (the lead doubts it is natural), and the tries a day stay as they were.
  - **A novelty is told, and a deed heard is tried more readily** (amended 25 November 2026, the project lead: "Je confirme et je suis d'accord pour toutes tes propositions, elles sont très bien"). The first runs showed a gesture reached the fire only inside a kept trial, and a kept trial rests on the food gathered and the distress days, which a sheet of bark does not change: a find was almost never told, and hearing never made anyone try sooner (the chance to start a trial reads only the will and the distress).
    - **A novelty is told** (`Record::Deed {body, day, deed, made, will}` in the well; the well is not saved, so no storage change): a deed of the hands that makes a kind of thing new to its doer (a flint taken for the first time, a sheet of bark, a folded one) is told at its next fire, before and apart from any judgment; the trial's kept or given-up verdict works as before. Listeners hold it as any heard way.
    - **A deed heard is tried more readily than an invention**: a body holding a heard deed of the hands it does not know (a novelty, or a kept trial of the hands) gets an extra daily chance to set out to try it: its usual chance by its will (in distress `try_percent`, else the calm per mille), never under the floor of every trial (`try_floor_permille`, 1 ‰, D50 amended the same day) times a factor by the different tellers it heard it from (`heard_factor_by_tellers`: ×5 for one, ×10 for two or three, ×20 for four or more), at most every journée. D30 placeholders in `content/needs.ron`, `trials`.
    - The trial so begun aims at every heard deed it does not know yet, so that pieces heard from several tellers can make a chain; it learns only those that come to something in its own hands. A deed set out to try is not drawn again on the extra chance until heard again after that journée. A kept heard way still comes first when the will alone makes it try, and the heard deeds it does not know come before a blind draw.
  - **What served is kept** (the project lead, 25 November 2026: "c'est propre d'un cerveau"): a thing that once worked for the body, a tool whose cut gave something or a container that held food beyond what the arms alone hold, is carried on and never let go when its hands are idle, whether or not a trial taught it a deed (`Handwork::served`, kinds of thing). It is the body's own memory of use, not a perception: nothing tells it what a thing is for before it served. Other things its known deeds do not use are let go when it is not trying its hands, as before.
  - **Told, heard, tried, never copied** (D55): a kept trial of the hands is a trial record like any other, told at the fire and retold. A listener holds the access; when its will makes it try (`heard_candidate`, the kept heard way first), it tries **the deeds heard** (`Handwork.aim`), as a body that knows them would, and learns those that come to something in its own hands; it fails when the flint or the birch is not at hand within its days. The hands are never copied by prestige (`imitate`), as the home is not. Mastery is counted on the mind (`Handwork.practised`, the times each deed came to something). There are no visits between camps yet: the other tribe hears nothing, and finds it alone or not.
  - **Amended on 25 November 2026 — the knowledge lot: a free hand, the tool the tale names, nothing judged without a try.** The project lead approved the audit `content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/audit-invention-savoir-2026-11-25.md` (sections 1, 2 and 6): "Non, c'est parfait, c'est même incroyable… C'est très très bien ce qu'ils ont suggéré. Je suis partant, on y va." The audit found that 97 % of the trials of the hands began on a tale and only ever redid the heard gestures (nearly always taking a stick, a stone or a flint), about eight true free trials a run; that every one of them that could do nothing was told as a failure; and that a listener who heard "cut a birch with a flint" never thought to pick a flint up. Three rules, each a true gesture or how a trial is judged:
    - **(1) A trial with nothing to try is not judged** (D50, amended the same day): no gesture its hands did not know at the trial's start begun in its days (`Handwork.gestured`, set when such a deed is begun: a pick-up, a cut, a fold), the trial ends unjudged, nothing told, the will untouched (`handwork::end_untried`, `News::Untried`).
    - **(2) A trial from a tale keeps a free hand**: in a trial of the hands, when no deed it knows or heard can be done here (none lacking what it gives, or its thing not within `hands.near_tiles`), the body draws a gesture as a trial on no tale does, among those the things at hand allow (`hands_turn`; before, it drew only when it had heard nothing). The heard deeds still come first.
    - **(3) The tool a deed names is taken up**: a cut it knows or heard names its tool ("cut a birch with a flint" holds the flint). With no thing of that material carried, a body lacking what the cut gives takes one lying within its hands' sight (`hands.near_tiles`, 4 tiles, the same sight as every gesture) for the cut, a birch in view or not; then the cut is done as before when a birch stands near. It is the content of the deed, not a steer: it never walks to look for a flint. The pick-up is an ordinary take: in a trial, a flint new to it is learnt as "take flint" as any take is, and kept by the hands' rule (`used`) or let go after a trial given up.
    - **Measured by the vivarium** (`docs/10`, `hands.tribes[].years[]`): `untried` (trials ended unjudged), `drew_free` and `drew_heard` (trials that drew a gesture, begun on no tale or on a tale: together the trials that truly tried something new), `tools_taken` (tools taken up for a cut); a person's trial ended unjudged carries `untried_day`. No run made with this change: the run is chosen with the lead.
    - **Decided alone, told**: what counts as having tried: a gesture *begun* (the world may then refuse it) on a deed its hands did not know at the trial's start, so a trial that only redid known deeds is one with nothing new to try; a heard deed set out to try in an untried trial is still marked tried (`tried_heard`), so the extra chance does not draw it again until heard again, and the trial slot is not taken again and again by the same tale; the tool is taken whether or not a tree of the cut's kind stands near, and for a cut known as well as one heard (a body that knows the cut and lost its flint takes up the next one it sees); the news of a drawn gesture is given once a trial; the tool taken up is counted when the pick-up is done, not when it sets out.
  - **The world judges, nothing steers**: no perception is added to lead a body to a container; with one, a body brings more home that day and its hunger feels it, or not.
  - **The vivarium measures it** (`docs/10`, section `hands`): the first container (when, by whom), the carriers by age-year, the makes, remakes and tears, the deeds told and who heard or missed them, who heard and never tried, the food brought home with and without a container, the noon distance from home and the homes changed by age-year, the deeds known at the end and the cuts by the tool in hand. A diagnostic beside the experiment, never in it: `--hands-known N` lets the first N grown founders of each tribe know the deeds of a container from birth (read from the content, `sim::brain::container_deeds`), to read what a container changes when one exists.
  - **Not now**: the player, the client (the protocol says nothing of the gestures yet; the flint on the ground reaches the client as any natural item, drawn as the pebbles are), images, cord, baskets, clothes, tents, fire.
  - D30 placeholders: `content/needs.ron` section `hands` (absent: nobody tries a gesture), the materials' and spawns' values. Storage: a body's gesture under way is in its snapshot only while it lasts (`making`, absent otherwise); no format change. Protocol unchanged.
  - **Amended on 25 November 2026 — folded bark lasts a cycle.** The project lead's principle E of the clocks audit (`content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/audit-horloges-2026-11-25.md`): a thing lasts at least as long as what it serves. Folded birch bark tore in 16 days (one season) while the nuts it carries keep 64; on the reference run of the same day 94 % of the boxes tore while carried and seed 1 remade 306 of them. `wears_days` is now **64**, a cycle.
  - **Amended on 25 November 2026 — no cut when the cut cannot be carried.** The bag study (`content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/etude-sac-2026-11-25.md`, defect 1, proposal 1) found 2.7 to 6.5 bark cuts per container made (seed 1: 2,932 barks for 449 containers). A cut made with the load full let the bark fall at the cutter's feet, and the cutter still lacked bark, so it cut again ten minutes later while it stood there (a mother carrying a baby and a full container fell into it easily). **What changes**: a body feels its load (a true perception, like the bark it sees on a tree). It does not choose a cut whose bark would not go on it: load plus the bark's mass above its capacity, the world's own test. This holds for a deed it knows or heard and for a gesture drawn in a trial (`brain::handwork::bark_fits`). The world is unchanged: a load filled during the gesture still lets the bark fall. The vivarium counts those (`hands.barks_dropped`, `docs/10`). **Decided alone, told**: (1) fixed in the brain, not as a refusal of the world; (2) the study's "a dropped bark sets the tree aside for the day" is not built, since a bark is no longer dropped by choice. Not verified on runs.
  - **25 November 2026 (D09 amended)**: `wears_days` stays for a short wear; folded birch bark's is written `wears_seasons`, 4 (64 days in calendar v1), never both.
  - **Amended on 26 November 2026 — folded bark lasts two seasons.** The project lead, on the long run of seed 5 (960 days, `87eb02e`): a box that lasts a whole cycle is too long. `wears_seasons` is now **2** (32 days in calendar v1). Reason: a container worn out sooner is remade sooner, so the gesture is practised and passed on more often (his remark: obsolescence pushes people to make again); it still outlasts a season of gathering. Principle E (a thing lasts as long as what it serves) bends here by his choice: the nuts keep four seasons, the box that carries them two. Storage and protocol unchanged.
  - **Amended on 26 November 2026 — a container sought from a lived problem (D50, amended the same day).** "Arms full" is remembered as a problem linked to `holds_g` in the content (`content/needs.ron`, section `problems`); a body its will minds to try it begins a trial of its hands at a free moment where the things around allow a gesture its hands do not know, there and then or later, its gesture drawn as before among what the things allow; that trial is judged over a season by whether a later gathering stopped full finds it carrying more than its arms alone: the same test as "what served is kept". No word for a bag or a box anywhere; told at the fire with its problem, tried first by a listener living the same problem.
  - **Amended on 27 November 2026 — the world alone: scales, false leads, a fold computed (step 1 of "acts and discovery").** The project lead asked how the box had been found; the study `content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/etude-actes-et-decouverte-2026-11-27.md` answered that it had been **whispered** at four places, and he approved its build order. Its test: a piece is legitimate if it can be written without knowing the hoped discovery, holds for many things, and allows being wrong. This step undoes the first two whispers and half of the third; the rest (steps 2 to 7) is not built.
    - **Scales, not uses.** `sharp`, `folds_into` and `holds_g` are gone from `content/materials.ron`, and so is the material `folded_birch_bark`. Every material has a value (0 when absent) on `hardness`, `edge`, `suppleness`, `toughness` (0 to 10), a `form` (`block`, `sheet`, `stick`, `strand`, `bits`) and, for a sheet, its face `area_dm2`. Nothing names what a thing is for.
    - **A cut computed** (`Content::cut_gives`, `Material::works`): the thing in hand detaches a tree's bark when it has an edge at least as keen as the bark is tough and is the harder of the two. A tree whose species names no bark still gives nothing.
    - **A fold computed** (`Material::folded`): what is no sheet does nothing; a sheet at least `hands.fold_suppleness` (5) supple takes a **hollow**, the open box of the most volume a square of its face folds into (2/27 of its side cubed: 4.74 litres for 16 dm²); a stiffer sheet **breaks and is lost** (`Event::Gestured.broke`). The hollow is a form of the thing, `Item.hollow_ml`, not another material; a hollow keeps `hands.loose_g_per_litre` (500 g) of loose food a litre on top of the arms, still by being carried (the "put in" gesture is step 2). Only a thing folded wears, from the fold (`wears_days`, `wears_seasons`). A thing folded is not folded again and never merges with another.
    - **False leads** (placeholders meant plausible, none chosen for what the people make of them): stones `quartz` (hardness 7, edge 5), `slate` (3, 4) and `sandstone` (4, 0) beside `flint` (7, 9) and `pebble` (6, 1); barks `birch_bark` (supple 7, tough 5, 16 dm², folded two seasons as before), `oak_bark` (stiff 1, tough 6: it breaks) and `pine_outer_bark` (stiff 1, tough 2: it breaks), from the oak and the pine of `content/species.ron`; `butterbur_leaf`, a big leaf of the banks (supple 9, 9 dm²: a hollow of 2 litres that tears a day after the fold). So flint cuts the three barks, quartz the birch and the pine, slate the pine alone, and the birch bark that held 4 kg by decree now holds the 2.37 kg its face gives. Five spawn rules are appended to `content/spawns.ron` (80 per thousand each), after the old ones so that the things of the old rules keep their ids.
    - **The draw reads what a body sees, and can fail** (`sim::brain::handwork`; the lead's rule of 25 November 2026, "on ne coupe pas avec une baie", kept and made general): a cut is drawn with any thing carried that shows an edge (`hands.edge_seen`, 3) on **any** tree near; a fold of any thing carried that is a sheet, stiff or not, or gives in hand (`hands.supple_seen`, 5). Whether the edge is keen enough for this bark, whether this tree's bark comes away, whether the sheet bends or snaps is not seen: the world answers. A gesture that gives nothing or breaks the thing is remembered as before: nothing learnt, nothing told, the trial judged on what its hands gained (no memory of failures yet: step 5).
    - **A kind of thing has a form**: in the hands' deeds and memories, a kind is a material's index with a bit when it is a hollow (`brain::HOLLOW`), so that "it lacks what the fold gave" still redoes the fold when the hollow tears.
    - **Still whispered, said**: what holds loose food holds by being carried; the label tying `full` to `holds_g` in `needs.ron` stays (read by no decision); a known cut is still not done when its bark could not be carried, but a cut drawn no longer foresees a bark it knows nothing of.
    - **Decided alone, told**: the open-box formula for every sheet, limp or stiff; wear only once folded; the two thresholds of what is seen; a hide given a sheet's face (1 m², nothing makes one yet); the leafy foods given a suppleness, so that folding them is plausible and gives nothing; the leaf as a thing the ground drops, since crops only give food.
    - **Measured by the vivarium** (`docs/10`): `hands.tried[]` (every cut and fold by what it was done on and with: gave, nothing, broke), `hands.made_of` (containers by material), `hands.first.material`.
    - **Storage changed, said**: `SNAPSHOT_FORMAT_VERSION` 20. An item may carry `hollow_ml`; a v19 world's `folded_birch_bark` items become birch barks with today's hollow (`migrate_v19_to_v20`). **No protocol change** (a hollow thing reaches the client as its material; the client shows nothing of the fold yet), **no dependency**.
  - **Amended on 27 November 2026 — "put in" is a gesture, nothing holds by itself; failures are remembered (step 2 of "acts and discovery", with the study's memory of failures brought forward).** The project lead ordered step 2 of the same study, and on the run of step 1 (seed 34, 256 journées, two tribes of 25: a first hollow at journée 19, a folded butterbur leaf, in both tribes; 109 birch hollows, 67 leaf ones) added the memory of failures, which the study kept for step 5: **nothing was remembered of what failed**. Oak bark was cut 299 times and broke at the fold 268 times, hazel was struck 386 times for nothing, and 15 people kept "cut an oak with a flint" as a known deed though it leads nowhere. This undoes the fourth whisper (the container that held without a gesture) and the label of the third. Steps 3 to 7 are not built, nor the similarity of step 5.
    - **A fourth gesture, "put A in B"** (`Gesture::PutIn`, `Intention::PutIn`, `sim::hands`): both carried, the content's gesture minutes whatever comes of it. The world answers from what the two things are: a thing with **no hollow holds nothing**; a hollow takes loose things up to its volume (`hands.loose_g_per_litre`, as in step 1); a **loose** thing (food, or a material in `bits`) goes in as far as the room allows, the rest staying in the arms; a whole thing goes in whole or not at all; a thing folded into a hollow, or lying in one already, does not go in. What lies in a hollow is the item's own `within` (the hollow's id), true while the same body carries both. Water is no thing yet (step 4).
    - **Carrying (D35 amended)**: the arms hold `movement.hands_g` of loose food, and **a hollow merely carried adds nothing**. Food put in a hollow is off the arms (`Body::stowed_g`, derived as the load is); gathering and picking up fill the arms alone, and stop when they are full. A hollow let go or torn **spills** what it held at the body's feet; a thing taken in hand, laid down or kept in a store is out of its hollow.
    - **Found as the other gestures are**: in a trial of its hands a put-in is drawn among the four gestures, of any thing carried in any other thing carried that **looks like it holds**: a thing folded into a hollow, or any sheet (`holds_to_the_eye`). On a flat sheet nothing stays: the draw can fail here too. The project lead's rule ("on ne coupe pas avec une baie") stays the filter: nobody puts a bark in a berry. A put-in that held, new to its hands, is told that evening as any novelty.
    - **Then done without a thought** (the engine's own "redo what it lacks"): a body that knows a put-in (learnt in its own trial, or in a trial begun on a tale) and whose **arms are full** puts their loose food in a thing it carries of the kind the deed names that has room left: it lacks what the put-in gave, room in its arms. A put-in it only heard is tried as soon as its arms hold any loose food and it carries such a thing.
    - **Failures are remembered** (`Handwork::failed`, `hands.failures_kept`, 16, a D30 placeholder): a cut, a fold or a put-in that gave nothing or broke the thing is remembered as its deed (the gesture, on what kind, with or in what kind), in a list of at most 16, the oldest forgotten when one more comes; nothing else fades it. A gesture so remembered is **not drawn again** (`Plausible::seen`), and does not count as something new to try. A failure of a deed its hands know is not remembered (it failed for the moment's sake: the hollow was full). Failures are **not told at the fire**: not built, the lead decides.
    - **Amended 27 November 2026, after the run of seed 35** (the project lead: yes to "the gesture works" is not "my problem is solved"). The run: 39 people found the put-in, about 13 still knew one at the end; 76 trials of 109 were given up, most on a leaf hollow torn before the judge came, and a trial given up took back every deed it had learnt. Now a trial of the hands **not kept** (given up, lapsed, judged no better) leaves known the deeds of which something came that served, by the same rule as a kept one (`handwork::leave_not_kept`, `keep_what_served`): the put-in of food in a kind of hollow that held food beyond the arms once, and the deeds that lead to it. The judge still says alone whether the problem is solved: such a trial is not kept, raises no will, is not told as kept, and its deeds are not noted as its own (`Handwork::kept`). Nothing served: its ways as before, as it was.
    - **A hollow that shows no room is not tried** (`World::hollow_shows_no_room`, read by `Plausible::seen`): no room left, or a whole thing heavier than the room left in a hollow that already holds something. The same run had 376 put-ins of a branch in a part-full bark hollow give nothing, one body 49 times, the deed being known and so never remembered as a failure. Whether a thing fits an empty hollow is still found by trying.
    - **Amended 27 November 2026, after the runs of seeds 37 and 38, read in the run's journal** (the project lead: yes to each). Three things. **Sixteen deeds** a body holds, six before (`handwork::DEEDS`, in memory only, nothing stored changes): a long trial that had filled its six with deeds serving nothing (a flint, a branch, a pebble put in its hollow) had no room to learn the put-in of food, and was given up while it carried more than its arms, five of the thirteen that put food in a hollow in seed 37; with sixteen, 29 of 96 knew a put-in of food at the end of seed 38, 6 of 100 before. Sixteen is still a ceiling: the deeds as records of the memory, with none, is a work of its own. **A trial from a problem that has just learnt a deed is given the trial's days from then** (`resolve`, `Aim::until`): a hollow folded and filled on the last evening of its season was given up that same night. **Take out** is a fifth gesture (`Gesture::TakeOut`, `Intention::TakeOut`, `World::take_out`), the put-in undone: a thing lying in a hollow carried comes out of it and stays carried; nothing comes of it when the thing lies in nothing, or is loose food the arms have no room for. In seed 38 a body kept its bark hollow full of the stones that had cut for it and gathered arms full beside it for fifteen days: nothing could empty a hollow. It is drawn among the plausible like the others (what lies in a thing carried is seen there), kept by the rule of what served when it made room in a kind of hollow a put-in of food kept is done in, and done again without a thought by a body that knows both, its arms full and no such hollow with room (`take_again`). **Storage, said**: no version change; `Gesture` gains a variant a stored gesture under way may name, older worlds name none. **No protocol change, no dependency.**
    - **Amended 27 November 2026, strike** (the project lead: yes to the gesture and to the flint that must be broken, the same day; "too heavy" as a problem and the single judge wait for a world where they have an answer: in the run of seed 39 that refusal was lived 1,213 times on a corpse and some 800 on what is likely a log, which no blow answers). **Strike** is a sixth gesture (`Gesture::Strike`, `Intention::Strike`, `World::strike`): a thing carried struck with another. The world answers on the scales (`Material::struck_with`): the less tough of the two breaks, the struck or the striker; neither when they are as tough, when one has no toughness (flesh, fruit), when the one that would break is supple enough to fold, or when the other is under a tenth of its mass (`STRIKE_MASS`: a pebble breaks a branch, not a log). A piece comes off, half of the thing, or, when the material names what it breaks into (`breaks_into`, a property of the stuff, not a use), a piece of that. **The flint lies as nodules** (`flint_nodule`, 1.2 kg, edge 2, under `edge_seen`: it does not look like it cuts, and does not): struck with the tougher pebble, keen flint comes off it, 400 g a blow; with a branch, nothing; a pebble struck with a nodule, the nodule breaks all the same. Quartz and slate keep their natural edge: a shorter way to the bark stays. A body draws a blow among the plausible as any gesture: any thing in the hand, with a thing one can hit with (a block or a stick, no food); the deed is kept by the rule of what served, when a deed kept is done with or on the piece (`product`). **Storage and frames, said**: no version change; `Gesture` gains a variant, the content a material at the end of the list and four spawn rules renamed, so the frames' print of the first generator is pinned again (same bytes, 789,834). **No protocol change, no dependency.** The client shows the nodule in its content colour.
    - **A deed stays known only if something came of it that served** (`handwork::keep_what_served`): when a trial of the hands is kept, of the deeds learnt in it only these stay: a put-in of food in a kind of thing that served (food lay in it while the body carried more than its arms alone hold: "what served is kept", now read on the food put in, not on the thing carried), and, step by step back from there, a deed whose product a deed kept is done on, with or in (the fold that gave the hollow, the cut that gave the sheet, the tool taken for the cut). The rest is forgotten as after a trial given up, before the trial is told: "cut an oak with a flint" no longer rides on a kept trial. The deeds it knew before the trial are not judged again. A novelty is still told the evening it is made, before any judgment, as the lead decided on 25 November.
    - **The judge is as it was** (D50): a trial from `full` is kept when a later gathering stopped full finds the body carrying more than its arms alone, which now takes food put in a hollow; the problem is lived when the arms are full and nothing it carries lies in a hollow, a hollow carried or not. **The label is gone**: `property` (`full: holds_g`, `felt`) is removed from `content/needs.ron` and from `ProblemKind`; the study had verified that no decision read it, and it named the answer.
    - **Decided alone, told**: (1) a put-in takes the gesture's ten minutes like a cut or a fold; (2) both things must be carried: a thing lying near is first picked up, which is the pick-up gesture, and a hollow lying on the ground holds nothing; (3) the room of a hollow is counted in grams at `loose_g_per_litre` for everything, a stone as berries (no density scale yet); (4) **the deed names the hollow, not the food**: "put berries in a birch hollow", once known, puts any loose food of the full arms in a birch hollow (the arms do not tell foods apart either), but a butterbur hollow asks for its own put-in to be found or heard (likeness between hollows is step 5); (5) a known put-in is redone when the arms are full, not at every handful; (6) what a hollow held falls to the ground when the hollow is dropped or tears (a leaf hollow tears in a journée); (7) failures fade only by the list's bound; (8) the diagnostic `--hands-known` gives its founders the put-in too (of the content's first food), since a hollow nothing is put in holds nothing.
    - **Measured by the vivarium** (`docs/10`, `hands.put_in` and `hands.tribes[].put_in`): the people who made a hollow, who of them then found the put-in and who never did, the journées between a person's first hollow and its first put-in, the put-ins by outcome, the gestures set aside for a failure remembered, the same failure repeated by a person (median and most), the deeds known at the end with how many hold them from a kept trial; `hands.tried[]` counts the put-ins by what was put in what. `problems.kinds[].property` is gone with the label. No run made with this change: the run is chosen with the lead.
    - **Storage changed, said**: `SNAPSHOT_FORMAT_VERSION` 21. An item may carry `within`, a put-in under way its `into`; a v20 world has nothing put in anything and reads the same (`migrate_v20_to_v21`, tested): a body that carried food beyond its arms beside a hollow holds it in its arms and takes no more till they have room. **No protocol change** (the gestures are not in the protocol; the load sent to the client is the whole load, unchanged), **no dependency**.
  - **Amended on 27 November 2026 — trees in parts: the trunk, the branches, the notch, the fall (the world only).** The project lead ordered it as the next step of "acts and discovery", with its rule: "ce ne sont pas des actes suggérés, mais des propriétés". Before, a tree gave only its bark to a cut, branches were dead wood on the forest floor, `log` was a material nothing produced, and no tree ever fell. Physics only: no "fell" gesture, no need for wood, no new problem, no rule that makes anyone insist on a tree, no use for chips or logs; whether anyone ever fells a tree is for the people to show.
    - **A tree has parts** (`content/needs.ron`, `hands.trees`, `content::TreeParts`; absent, a tree is as before). A **trunk** of a thickness: its kind's height over its slenderness (`slender` 80, `slender_big` 40 for the `big` kinds of `species.ron`: a hazel stem 6 cm, a birch 22 cm, an oak 55 cm), each tree thinner or thicker by up to `girth_spread_percent` (25), fixed for the tree by the seed and where it stands (`World::trunk_mm`); nothing is added to `species.ron`. Living **branches**. **Living wood** is its own stuff on the scales (`green_branch`: hardness 2, suppleness 5, toughness 6; dead `branch` 3, 2, 5), with `wood_chips` (bits) at the end of `materials.ron`.
    - **A cut names its part** (`hands::Part`: `trunk`, `branch`; `Intention::Cut.part`, `Making.part`, `Event::Gestured.part`). In the brain a deed names it as it names a hollow, a flag on the kind (`handwork::BRANCH` on the species' index; the trunk is the deed of before), and both are seen as plausible on every tree near with every thing that shows an edge, drawn among the plausible as cuts were: nothing says which part. **On the trunk**: the bark, as before, when the tool works it; a tool that works the living wood (`Material::works`: only keen flint among what lies about) **bites**: it cuts through `bite_mm2` (330 mm²) of the trunk's section for each point of its edge over the wood's toughness, plus one (flint: 1,320 mm² a gesture of ten minutes), chips fall at the cutter's feet (`chips_g_per_cm2` 5: 66 g a gesture, joining the pile lying there), and the tree's **notch** deepens (`ChunkChanges::notched`, by tree, the sum of every cutter's cuts; it lasts). A trunk whose bark does not come away gives its chips. **On a branch**: a tool that works living wood takes a green branch off (2 kg), carried, or at the feet when too heavy.
    - **A tree falls when its notch has cut its trunk's whole section through**, not before (`World::fell`, `Event::TreeFell`): 2 gestures for a stem of 5 cm, 6 (an hour) for 10 cm, 31 for a birch of 22 cm, 96 (16 hours of work) for an oak of 40 cm, 180 for one of 55 cm. The time grows as the square of the thickness, as the felling experiments with hafted stone axes give (about 20 to 28 cm² of section a minute; the sources are in `content/needs.ron`); no measured figure was found for a flake held in the bare hand, so the bite is set on the project lead's own figure (a thin tree in the order of an hour, a big oak a matter of days): a placeholder to that extent. A gesture keeps the content's minutes. **Fallen**, it is among its chunk's `felled` trees for good (a felled forest never grows back by itself): it stands in no list of trees, blocks its tile no more, bears nothing (`World::ripe_on` and its kin), gives nothing to a cut; where it stood lie a **log** of its trunk's mass (section × the kind's height × `stem_form_percent` 45 × `wood_kg_per_m3` 900: 6 kg for a hazel stem, 290 kg for a birch, 2.1 t for an oak; one thing, far beyond the 20 kg a body carries, except a hazel's) and the branches of its crown big enough to be things (`crown_percent` 20 of the trunk's mass in pieces of 2 kg, one at least, `branches_most` 8 at most).
    - **The ground is patched, never placed anew** (`Ground::set_felled`, with D51's trees placed once): the felled trees are laid over the seed's trees held, the chunk's trunks and walkable pieces worked out again. The seed's trees stay held beside (`Ground::seed_trees_in`): the plants and the bearers are still placed from them, so no plant moves when a tree falls and the ground kept on disk between vivarium runs is the seed's alone.
    - **Frames, within the protocol as it is**: a chunk's frame made after a tree fell carries the trees still standing, at the chunk's new version (`Shared::tree_changed`); the log and the branches go as items added. **Not done, because it would change the protocol**: nothing tells a client that already holds the chunk; it keeps drawing the tree until the chunk is sent to it again. **No protocol change, no client change, no dependency.**
    - **Storage schema changed, said**: `CHUNK_CHANGES_FORMAT_VERSION` 3 (2 before): a chunk's changes gain a sixth list, the notches (the tree's key, the square millimetres cut). Formats 1 and 2 are still read, with no notch (`chunk_changes_of_format_2_are_read_with_no_notch`); a notch of a felled tree or two of one tree are refused as damage. The world's snapshot keeps its version: a cut under way names its part only when it is a branch, older worlds name none. The frames' print of the first generator is unchanged.
    - **Decided alone, told**: (1) one living wood for every kind of tree; (2) the trunk's thickness from the kind's height and `big`, no field per species, and a spread of a quarter each way; (3) the bite grows with the edge's keenness over the wood's toughness; (4) the fall asks for the whole section cut (a real tree goes when a hinge is left); (5) chips fall on the ground at the cutter's feet, not in its hands (carried, a body dropped them at once, one more act a cut); (6) one log a tree; (7) a standing tree gives branches without end, none counted off it; (8) a fallen tree's log and branches lie on its own tile; (9) `green_branch` does not feed a fire. **A consequence to know**: living wood being tougher (6) than flint (5), a flint or a nodule struck with a green branch breaks, by the strike rule as it stood (a dead branch, toughness 5, breaks nothing): a second way to keen flint, a wooden hammer, which the lead may want or not.
    - **Measured by the vivarium** (`docs/10`, `hands.trees`): the cuts by part, the bites, the chips, the branches taken, the trees felled (who, what species, how thick, the day).
  - **Amended on 28 November 2026 — bark apart from wood, and a tree that heals (the world only).** The project lead validated both and wants trees left alone after that ("j'ai peur qu'on aille dans le trop complexe"). Defect: every cut of bark with flint also deepened the notch, so a birch barked 31 times fell. This replaces, in the amendment above, "the bark, as before, when the tool works it; a tool that works the living wood bites" and "it lasts".
    - **Bark is finite and lifting it never deepens the notch.** A trunk wears so many pieces of its bark where the hands reach: its girth times `bark_reach_mm` (1,000, `hands.trees`, a D30 placeholder) over the area of one piece (`area_dm2` of the bark in `materials.ron`), one at least (`TreeParts::bark_plates`: 4 on a birch of 22 cm, 10 on an oak of 55 cm). While a piece is left, a cut on the trunk with a thing that works the bark lifts one (`Event::BarkLifted`) and bites nothing; a thing that does not work it gets nothing.
    - **The wood is cut only where the bark is gone.** Bare of its bark, the trunk answers the same `cut` gesture with its wood: chips and the notch to a thing that works living wood, nothing to another (quartz on a stripped birch).
