A kind whose bark does not come away (hazel) gives its chips at once, as before. `Content::cut_gives` says what a whole tree gives; the state of a tree is the world's (`World::trunk_now`, `World::bark_left`). No new gesture, nothing told to the brain about what to do.
    - **A tree heals.** A notch nobody deepens closes, as if the whole section closed in `notch_closes_seasons` (8: two cycles; a lesser notch in proportion: one bite of flint in a birch of 22 cm in 5 journées, a birch half cut through in a cycle; a felling of several journées loses about a fifth of a bite a night); the bark grows back piece by piece, all of it in `bark_grows_seasons` (8). Both are D30 placeholders in `hands.trees`. Once both are back to nothing the tree is as the seed made it and nothing is kept of it. **A felled tree stays felled.**
    - **Nothing scales with the map.** A marked tree keeps its notch and its bark lifted as they were on the journée of the last mark (`changes::Scar` in `ChunkChanges::scars`, which replaces `notched`), with that journée and the journée by which it will be whole. What is left today is worked out when the tree is looked at or cut (`World::trunk_now`), never written by looking, never by a pass over the trees. A healed record is dropped when men next change its chunk (`World::change`) and when the chunk is loaded (`World::restore_changes`), by its date alone, its own chunk's marks alone looked at; the chunk's version does not move for it.
    - **The brain kept truthful, by what a body sees** (`brain::handwork`). A deed's product stays what a whole tree gives. (1) A body that cuts for the bark it lacks, by a deed it knows or heard, does not choose a trunk that shows its wood bare (`World::bark_left`): otherwise it cut the same stripped birch without end. (2) A cut answered by a bare trunk with another thing than the deed gives on a whole tree (chips, or nothing) is the tree's answer, not the deed's: nothing is learnt, practised or told of the deed, and it is not remembered as a gesture that gives nothing (`Handwork::gave`, the thing that came). The gestures drawn in a trial are unchanged: any tree near, either part. `hits_to_the_eye` reads the form of a material alone and assumes nothing of a tree.
    - **Storage schema changed, said**: format 3 of the chunk changes, which exists only in this unmerged pull request, is itself reshaped (`CHUNK_CHANGES_FORMAT_VERSION` stays 3): its sixth list holds, for each marked tree, its key, the square millimetres cut, the pieces of bark lifted, the journée of the last mark and the journées from it to the one it is whole. Formats 1 and 2 are still read, with no marked tree; a mark on a felled tree, two on one tree or a mark of nothing are refused as damage. The world's snapshot keeps its version. **No protocol change, no client change, no dependency.**
    - **Decided alone, told**: (1) all the bark where the hands reach comes off before any wood is cut, no place on the trunk is kept; (2) under a bark the thing in hand cannot lift, no edge reaches the wood; (3) healing is linear, in proportion to the tree (the section, the count of pieces), written in seasons as the other long durations; (4) the journée of the last mark restarts both healings, so a tree marked every journée regrows no bark; (5) the record carries the journée it is whole, worked out with the content of the journée of the mark, so that it can be dropped without the content; (6) a bark lifted is told by its own event, which the server needs to follow the chunk's version; (7) the two points of the brain above; (8) trees without parts (`hands.trees` absent) give bark without end, as before them.
  - **Amended on 28 November 2026 — seen is not kept: a success is a lived problem relieved, and only what is kept is done again.** The project lead, on the audit of the trials (`content/ai-reserve/review/lot-5/audit-essais-2026-11-28.md`, the run of seed 41: 20,110 gestures, 75 % of them in a trial and 6 % of those leading to food put in a hollow; 8,239 trunk cuts of 9,678 a cut once seen done again; 738 trees felled by 28 people): "We want them to try, to understand successes, to remember failures. A success they want to keep, they keep and redo again and again. But success must be well defined: if it is 'I got bark' and that bark is good for nothing, it has no interest. Humans are lazy." He validated rules 1 to 3 of the audit and its two code defects; rules 4 and 5, effort and the fetching of material are not built.
    - **The cause.** A gesture of a trial that gave a thing entered the deeds the hands know at once, and a deed known is done again whenever the body does not carry what it gives. A trunk with no bark to lift answers a cut with chips, chips always fall at the cutter's feet, so the body lacked them for ever and cut at every thought. "Known in a trial, not yet judged" and "kept" were one list (`Ways::hands`) treated alike.
    - **Rule 1 — seen is not kept** (`Handwork::seen`, `hands.seen_kept`, 32, a D30 placeholder). In a trial of the hands a gesture that gives a thing leaves a memory, "this gives that", the oldest forgotten beyond the bound. It is not a deed of `Ways::hands`: those are from now on only the deeds **kept**. The thing stays in hand for the time of the trial, as before; the novelty is told that evening, as before.
    - **Rule 2 — a success is a lived problem relieved** (`handwork::keep_what_served`, called at the end of every trial of the hands). Today the one such problem with an answer is the full arms: food lay in a thing while the body carried more than its arms alone hold. Then the put-in of food in that kind of thing is kept, and step by step back from it the deeds seen in the trial whose product a kept deed is done on, with or in (the fold that gave the hollow, the cut that gave the bark, the blow or the taking up that gave the tool). Nothing else is kept; the rest stays seen. **A tool served inside such a chain**: the tool of a cut so kept is marked as having served; before, any cut that gave a thing marked its tool, never let go again (twelve flints on one person).
    - **Rule 3 — only what is kept is done again for its product's sake** (`handwork::wanted`). The deeds the hands do again when the body lacks what they give are: the deeds kept; in a trial, the deeds heard and aimed at that have not yet given a thing in this trial (each is tried, once), and the deeds that gave a thing in this trial and answer a problem (a put-in of loose food); and, step by step back from those, the deeds only seen or heard whose product one of them needs. A deed only seen whose product feeds none of them is never done again by the hands: it may be drawn again, four gestures a journée at most. So the chain flake, bark, hollow, food put in is still found inside one trial by draws, and from the first put-in of food that holds, the trial makes its hollow again when it lacks it. True of the six gestures alike; no case for trees.
    - **A trial cut short by a move followed ends as a trial not kept** (`Brains::start_move`): what served stays kept, what it was after is dropped. Before, the hands were put back as before the trial (a hollow that had served was lost), and the deeds heard stayed its aim with no trial, done again without end (twelve bark cuts in eleven journées for one person of the run of seed 40).
    - **The judgment is pushed once** (`ProblemAim::last`). A trial from a problem that sees a new deed near its end still has `trial_days` to live its problem again with it, but never later than its time and those days once (16 + 5 journées for the full arms). Before, each deed learnt pushed it again: a trial lasted 33 journées in the run of seed 41.
    - **What this replaces in the text above.** "A body redoes the deeds it knows when it lacks what they give" (the plain minimum) and "then done without a thought" now read "the deeds it keeps", with the trial's own exception above. "A deed stays known only if something came of it that served" was true at the trial's end only; it is now true at every moment, the deeds of a trial under way being seen, not known. "What served is kept: a tool whose cut gave something" now reads "the tool of a cut of a chain kept". D50's guard of four gestures a journée bounded only the draws; with rule 3 nothing else is done in a trial for a product that leads nowhere. The amendment of 28 November "bark apart from wood" called itself "the world only": it also changed what the hands learnt (a trunk with no bark gives chips, so a deed once known); rule 1 closes that.
    - **Still open, not settled by this round** (the audit's rules 4 and 5, the lead has not decided): a novelty heard still opens a trial to any listener, with a lived problem or not (this decision, 25 November), which D50 (26 November) contradicts for a kept trial told with its problem; the failures remembered are still a list of `failures_kept` (16) that turns over, so a failure is tried again often; a deed that gave a thing of no use is not remembered against its problem and may be drawn again in the next trial; a body that keeps an answer and lacks its material still begins a new trial instead of fetching it.
    - **Decided alone, told**: (1) what was seen is remembered beyond the trial, a bounded list of 32, the oldest first out; only the deeds that gave a thing in the trial under way may be kept at its end; (2) a deed seen in an earlier trial is no aim of a later one by itself: only a put-in of food that held in *this* trial makes its chain made again; (3) a deed heard and aimed at is tried until it gives a thing once in the trial, then it is a deed seen like any other; (4) the judgment's push keeps its trigger (any deed seen for the first time in the trial) and gains a ceiling, rather than the audit's narrower trigger (only a put-in of food), which would have given no days to a hollow folded on the last evening; (5) a deed that fails after having given a thing in the same trial is not remembered as a failure, as a deed known was not; (6) what the hands keep is said in the news at their next thought (`News::DeedKept`), the judges having no news of their own to give it in.
    - **Measured by the vivarium** (`docs/10`, `hands.work`): the gestures ended in a trial of the hands and outside, and of each those that led to food put in a hollow; the deeds seen (`News::DeedSeen`, once a trial) and kept; the pieces of bark lifted; the trees felled. `tools/lab/journal.py FILE gestures` prints the same from a run's journal, which now writes a line for each piece of bark lifted.
    - **No protocol or storage change**: the hands of a mind (`Handwork`) are in no snapshot and no message. `content/needs.ron` gains `hands.seen_kept`; `News` gains `DeedSeen` and `DeedKept`, read by the vivarium only.

  - **Amended on 28 November 2026 (second round) — a deed that does not give what was told is remembered; the hands are free for a gesture.** Two rules the project lead validated, with D50's single verdict of the same day.
    - **Not as told — the cause found.** In the run of seed 42 person 56 cut lindens, alders and willows 18 to 36 times a journée from journée 244 to 256. `shows_bare` never concerned her: those species have no bark that comes away (`content/species.ron`), so her cuts were not cuts for bark. They gave wood chips, as told; she did them for a deed heard that chips feed (chips put in a bark hollow, seen by 13 people of that run), and chips always fall at the cutter's feet: she lacked them for ever, and cut at every thought. So the rule is wider than "it gave another thing".
    - **The rule** (`Handwork::amiss`, `hands.amiss_kept`, 32, a D30 placeholder). A deed done for what it gives (a deed kept, heard, or one that feeds them: `handwork::needed`) that **did not leave that thing in the body's hands** (nothing came, another thing came, or it fell at its feet) is remembered of that very thing: "told bark, here it gave none" (`News::NotAsTold`). It is not done again on that tree or that thing; on another it may be. General to the six gestures, no special case for trees. The memory is a bounded list, the oldest forgotten; a tree remembered so stays so after it healed, until pushed out.
    - **What it does not cure, told**: such a body still cuts each such tree near it once. The full cure, picking up what fell at its feet when it is what it sought, was not validated and is not built.
    - **Free hands — the project lead's words**: "Avec les bras pleins, les mains ne sont pas libres : on ne peut pas faire une action. Il pose la chose, fait son essai, et la reprend ensuite, s'il n'oublie pas. Et s'il y a plusieurs choses qu'il veut prendre, à lui de choisir laquelle il préfère prendre."
    - **The world's rule** (`World::in_the_way`, `sim::Refusal::HandsFull`). A gesture other than a pick-up (cut, fold, put in, take out, strike) is refused to a body that carries anything but the gesture's own things: the tool, the thing worked, the hollow and what goes in it. A pick-up stays possible with loaded arms.
    - **A hollow laid down keeps what it holds** (`World::set_down_inside`): its contents lie on the tile `within` it, and picking the hollow up takes them along (the whole weight is checked); a thing picked out of it by itself is out of it. This replaces "what a hollow held falls with it" (27 November) for a hollow laid down; a hollow torn still spills. Reason: without it a container was worth nothing more under this rule; with it a full hollow is **one** laying and one taking back, where loose food is one a pile (tested: 1 against 3).
    - **The brain** (`handwork::act_on`, `take_back`). Before a gesture the body lays what is in the way at its feet, one thing a thought, each a real `Drop` with a drop's time (`News::Laid`), then does the gesture. When its hands want nothing more it takes back what it laid and still sees, one thing a thought, **in the order of its existing preferences**: food when it is hungry, then a thing of a kind that served it, then a thing a deed it keeps uses or gives, then food, then, in a trial, the rest; what none of these names is let be (`News::LaidLetBe`), and what no longer fits its arms too. Nothing forces the taking back and nothing is forgotten by chance: a move followed, a flight, the night interrupt as before and the things stay (`News::LaidLeft`); another may take them (`News::LaidLost`). It takes back only within its hands' sight (`near_tiles`), never by walking back; food laid is in its memory of food as any food seen, so hunger may bring it back.
    - **Kept deeds outside a trial follow the same rule**: the gesture is refused by the world to all alike.
    - **Decided alone, told**: (1) the world enforces the rule, the brain obeys it, so a player's gesture is refused too (shown as the protocol's existing `Unknown` refusal: no protocol change); (2) the laid hollow that holds, above; a snapshot may now hold a ground item `within` a ground hollow, the same field, no format change; (3) a mind is woken at the next tick after any pick-up or drop of its hands, else nothing woke it between a laying and its gesture; (4) what it laid is remembered in a list of `hands.laid_kept` (32, a D30 placeholder), pruned when the thing no longer lies there; (5) a tool laid down is often taken up again by its kept pick-up deed rather than by the taking back; (6) food piles are taken back whole or not at all.
    - **Measured by the vivarium** (`docs/10`, `hands.work`): `not_as_told`; `free_hands`: laid, taken back (and late), left, taken by another, gone, let be. `tools/lab/journal.py FILE gestures` prints them.
    - **No protocol, storage-format or dependency change.** `content/needs.ron` gains `hands.amiss_kept` and `hands.laid_kept`; `News` gains `NotAsTold`, `Laid`, `LaidBack`, `LaidLeft`, `LaidLost`, `LaidLetBe`.
  - **Amended on 28 November 2026 (third round) — a known result is not tried again, and the memory of results lasts.** The project lead, on the run of seed 42 (of 996 trunk cuts, 875 were made in 424 trials begun on a tale by people who lived no problem; 4,938 gestures, 6 kept): "A deed heard is tried more readily, yes. But one tries, and if it does not work and brings nothing, one stops. One can take it up again later. And then one can associate ideas: fire works with wood; cutting a tree gives wood; I will cut to get wood. That, one can remember. One remembers the result, one remembers that it brought nothing, but one can also make the link. I cut wood, for now it is useless, I stop. Three weeks later, a year later, fire is found, and fire needs wood, and I know that cutting a tree gives wood: it is in my memory." He **refused the audit's rule 5** (no trial without a lived problem): curiosity born of a tale stays.
    - **The cause, confirmed in the code and the journals.** Nothing told a body that it already knew what a deed gives. `heard_deeds` left out only the deeds kept and those tried since they were last told, so a deed seen was an aim again each time it was told again, and an aim is done until it gives a thing once in the trial; the draw set aside only the gestures that had given nothing, so a deed seen was drawn again in every trial. In the journals 40 % of the deeds "seen for the first time in a trial" were seen again by a body that had seen them in an earlier trial (1,189 of 3,001 in the run of seed 42; 1,720 of 4,072 in that of seed 43). And the memory was a list of 32, the oldest pushed out: 15 people of the run of seed 42 and 27 of that of seed 43 saw more than 32 different deeds in four cycles (50 at most), so the oldest were forgotten and new again.
    - **Rule 1 — a known result is not tried again** (`Handwork::knows`, `handwork::sift`, `Plausible::seen`). A body knows a deed's result when, before the trial under way, it saw it give a thing and did not keep it (no chain of it served a lived problem: `Handwork::seen`), or saw it give nothing (`Handwork::failed`). Such a deed (a) is **no aim of a tale**: hearing it told again does not aim its hands at it, whether by the extra chance of a deed heard, by a kept trial heard that its will tries, or by a trial from a problem aimed at the deeds told with that problem; (b) is **set aside by the draw**, as a remembered failure was. **A tale whose every deed the listener knows opens no trial** (`brain::weigh_tale`, `News::TaleKnown`); a tale with something new to it opens one, aimed at what is new alone. True of the six gestures alike, the taking up of a thing among them; no case for trees.
    - **"A deed heard is tried more readily" (25 November 2026) stays true, of what the listener does not know.** The extra chance, its factor by the tellers and its roll are unchanged, and are worked out on the whole tale as before; only then is the tale weighed against the listener's memory. The tale is noted as weighed that evening either way, and comes back to it only when told again.
    - **Rule 2 — except rarely** (`handwork::Again`, `hands.again_permille`, 10, a D30 placeholder). Each journée, for each person and each deed whose result it knows, 10 chances in 1,000, drawn from the run's seed for that body, that journée and that deed (`scatter::purpose::HANDS_AGAIN`), that it is ready to take that deed up again: that journée a tale aims at it and a draw does not set it aside (`News::TakenUpAgain`). In person-journées: one journée in 100 for a given person and a given deed, a cycle and a half of 64 journées; a person who knows 26 results (the median seen in the run of seed 43) has one of them open about one journée in four, and acts on it only if it then draws it in a trial or hears it told that evening.
    - **Rule 3 — the memory of results lasts.** One entry a deed, six bytes (the gesture, the kind it is done on, the kind it is done with or in), in one of two lists that never both hold a deed: what gave a thing that brought nothing (`seen`), what gave nothing (`failed`); a deed kept is in neither, it is in the ways (`Ways::hands`, 16). Nothing pushes an entry out in a life. **Weight**: in the two runs of four cycles a person saw 18 and 26 different deeds give a thing at the median and 50 at most, and had about 9 gestures give nothing at the median and 23 at most (counted from the journals without the tool's kind, so a floor): some 200 bytes a person at the median, under 500 at most. **What still bounds it**: the deeds the content allows (kinds of thing times kinds of thing, nothing of the map, of the people or of time), and the gestures of a life (four draws a journée at most, plus the deeds told); and a guard, `seen_kept` and `failures_kept`, 1,024 each (12 KB a person at the very most, the oldest going beyond): no life reaches it, it is there so that a defect cannot grow a mind without end. The two values were 32 and 16 and are no longer rules of the game.
    - **A result known is a result known, and the three memories stay apart.** "This gives nothing" (`failed`: before, a list of 16 that turned over, so a failure was tried again often) now lasts as "this gives a thing that brought me nothing" does, and both are set aside alike, the rare chance alike; a deed seen to give a thing that fails one day stays known by the thing, and a gesture that gave nothing and gives a thing at last is known by the thing. "Told bark, here it gave none" (`amiss`, second round above) is not a result of the deed but of one tree or one piece: it stays a list of 32 that turns over, because the things it names pass (a tree heals, a piece is eaten) and their number grows with the things met, not with the deeds.
    - **Rule 4 — the link by what a deed gives, from that memory** (`handwork::wanted`, rule 3 of the first round, unchanged in its principle): a deed seen is done again, however long ago it was seen and with no tale, when a deed kept or the chain in hand needs what it gives. Tested: a body saw long ago that a cut gives bark, has seen a hundred other things since, and the day a deed it keeps needs bark it goes and cuts.
    - **Not in this round** (the lead's list): resemblance by matter ("chips are wood"), fire, effort and fatigue, picking up what fell at the feet, fetching far material.
    - **Decided alone, told**: (1) a deed that gave a thing **in the trial under way** is not yet a known result: that trial's end says whether it served, so within one trial it may still be drawn again (four gestures a journée at most), as before; (2) **the link from a lived problem**: in a trial begun from a problem it lives, a deed it remembers having seen that answers a problem (today a put-in of food that held) is what the trial is after, and its chain is made again from memory, step by step; and such a trial begins when that chain can be worked here, even with nothing new around (`handwork::something_new`). Without it rule 1 took away what the draws did by chance: a body that had seen food hold in a hollow at a time its arms were not full could no longer find it again when they were; (3) the taking up of a thing is a deed like the others: once its result is known it is no longer drawn (this is what "the six gestures alike" means, and its cost is told below); (4) the things a remembered deed needs are not let go while a deed kept needs its product (`let_go`): else the tool was taken up for the cut and let go again without end; (5) the rare chance is one draw a body, a journée and a deed, the same for the tale and for the draw; (6) a tale all known that its will would have tried as a kept trial heard (`start_trial`) opens no trial by that tale, and its will draws a way as if it had heard nothing; (7) a trial from a problem begins by its problem whatever the listener knows of the deeds told with it: only its aim is sifted; (8) the guard above, 1,024, the oldest going beyond, rather than no bound at all.
    - **What it costs, told, not cured**: invention by draws from one trial to the next is now narrow. A body that took up a flint in a trial and made nothing of it does not draw that taking up again in its next trial, so it cannot draw a cut with it either: what was found by chance over several trials must now be found inside one trial, or come by the rare chance, by a tale with something new, or by the link from a problem. This is the rule as decided; the remedy that would keep both (a known deed is drawn again when its product would allow a gesture whose result the body does not know yet: "I never tried to fold this") is a new rule of the game and is **not built**. Likewise a blow that gave nothing because the striker was too light is now remembered as long as any failure.
    - **Measured by the vivarium** (`docs/10`, `hands.work.known_results`): the tales that opened no trial because all was known, the gestures set aside at draws as known, the rare takings up again (heard, drawn), and the size of the memory of the living at the end (median and most, in results and in bytes). `tools/lab/journal.py FILE gestures` prints the same counts from a journal, and the deeds seen and not kept a person.
    - **No protocol, storage or dependency change**: the hands of a mind (`Handwork`) are in no snapshot and no message, and the minds live in the vivarium only (D50). `content/needs.ron` gains `hands.again_permille`, and `hands.seen_kept` and `hands.failures_kept` go from 32 and 16 to 1,024 (their ceiling in the content's validation from 64 to 4,096); `News` gains `TaleKnown` and `TakenUpAgain`, and `Skipped` gains `known`, read by the vivarium only.

### D58 — Food pooled at the hearth: one reserve a camp, the young first
- **Reason**: the project lead, 25 November 2026, validating « la mise en commun » (`content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/mise-en-commun-2026-11-25.md`) with three corrections: the sharing is **at the hearth** (« le foyer », never « le feu »), everyone who lies down at the camp's evening gathering, not the family; a child is fed by the hearth **until eight**, not six ("un enfant de 2 ans ne se nourrit pas seul": D47 weaned it at two to gather for itself); and two true perceptions, both approved: a body sees its hearth's reserve at the camp, and the hunger of its own (its spouse, its children under sixteen). The same day he added **the young first**, a human trait he asked to have coded: when the reserve is short the young eat first, and a grown body carries an innate care for the young's coming days, stronger for its own children. Until now each ate what it gathered; nothing brought back served anybody else.
- **Consequences**:
  - **The hearth** is the circle of D55 (amended 24 November 2026: the home's place, `place_tiles`), keyed by the place's middle. Pooling rides on it: with the fire off (`--no-fire`) there is no hearth and nothing is pooled.
  - **One reserve a hearth, at the camp** (`sim::brain::pooling`): heaps of food lying on the ground within a tile of one tile, the home of the first who put food there. They are the world's own items (D35): they spoil there as food does anywhere (D45, `keeps_days`, a heap as old as its oldest matter), and are eaten with the world's own gesture. The brain only remembers which heaps are the hearth's, so that none takes them for food lying about; nobody of another hearth eats them (giving outside the hearth is not built). The world does not change: no intention, no event, no stored field.
  - **Carrying for its own.** While a body owes its hearth (what it set out at dawn to gather for it, below, less what it put down), of each gather its own's share, `owed / (store + owed)`, at most what it still owes, is carried for the hearth: **not its own to eat** on the way; hungry, it eats only what it carries beyond it. Its day's store is its own store plus what it owes. **Decided in the build, told**: the bodies come home at nightfall with half a day's food and eat it on the way, never holding the three days of their store (the review's `evening`, 0.5 of a day's need on seed 1 at richness 1.0); "beyond its store" alone put down 9.9 MJ in six days for fifty grown bodies of a trial run (0.03 % of their need), and the young of the hearth, who no longer gather, would have starved. With the share carried for its own, 138 MJ in the same six days.
  - **Putting down.** Back at its camp (home from the day, or at night), **before it eats**, a body lays down there, a heap a thought, what spoils first first, what it carries for its hearth and what it carries beyond its store (the stock way, `STOCK_KJ`, about three days). A heap goes down when most of it is beyond what it keeps.
  - **Eating.** Hungry at its camp (within `HOME_TILES` of its home, or on its home's place), a body eats from its hearth's reserve before its own, what spoils first first, walking to the heap. **The young first**: a grown body eats there only what the reserve holds beyond what the young of the hearth (under sixteen, seen at dawn) can still eat today (`room_j`, their true appetite); a child from `fed_until_years` to sixteen only beyond what the younger ones can; the younger always. A reserve it could not reach is left for the day.
  - **Amended on 25 November 2026 — the hunger bar.** The project lead: "Avoir faim et porter de la nourriture pour l'amener aux enfants, c'est ok. Avoir très faim et porter de la nourriture pour les enfants la garder, ça va être plus rare, et ça peut être en fonction de la volonté. Quelqu'un qui n'a pas de volonté va manger cette nourriture, quelqu'un qui a beaucoup de volonté va la ramener. Par contre, avoir très très faim, mourir de faim et avoir de la nourriture, ça personne ne le fera jamais, on mange la nourriture." Measured before it (PR #358, five seeds, richness 1.0): 98 of 252 hunger deaths carried food for their hearth they would not eat, and 38 grown bodies died at their camp beside a reserve (5.3 MJ median) the young first kept from them. How hungry a hungry body is (its hunger gauge is full for days before it dies) is read by **the share of its fat reserve left**: what is left of it (`Condition::reserve_left_j`) against its whole reserve (`Condition::reserve_j`, the content's to its size). From `very_hungry_fat_percent` (50) up, **hungry**: it carries its hearth's food home and leaves the young first, as above. Below, **very hungry**: it eats what it carries for its hearth, and from the reserve despite the young, unless its will keeps it, by `keep_percent_by_will` (the weak-willed eat, the strong-willed carry it home); the chance is drawn once a game day from the run's seed, its id and the day (salt `HUNGER_BAR`), the same draw for both, so that it does not change its mind each thought. Below `starving_fat_percent` (25), **starving**: it eats it, always. "Eaten" is the world's eating gesture, what a hungry body does with its own food: of what it carries, the heap that spoils first; from the reserve, as above. Eaten on the way, its hearth's mark (`hearth_carried_kj`) is lowered to what it still holds, and what it ate it owes its hearth again (**decided in the build, told**: only after a meal the bar gave, so that without the bar nothing changes; the hungry who eat their own share from a heap bigger than it keep the mark as before). A child the hearth feeds is not concerned: nobody is ahead of it. No perception is added: a body feels its own hunger. D30 placeholders in `needs.ron`, section `pooling`: `very_hungry_fat_percent` 50, `starving_fat_percent` 25, `keep_percent_by_will` [(0, 10), (100, 90)]; absent, nobody is more than hungry, as before. Measured: the vivarium's `pooling.hunger_bar` (the meals the bar gave, on the way and despite the young, very hungry and starving).
  - **The young fed by the hearth.** Weaned and under `fed_until_years` (8), a child of a hearth does not gather alone, go looking for food, nor follow a group of the fire's decisions (D56); it drinks, sleeps and moves home as before. From eight it gathers, puts down and eats at the hearth like the others. **A child is of a hearth for the day when it lay down in its camp's circle the night before** (before the first dawn, every child is): one that slept away from its camp has no hearth that day and fends for itself, gathering as before pooling. **Decided in the build, told**: on seed 1 two infants of tribe B were weaned on their mother's way home, 200 tiles from the camp, where her own walk home stopped short every day (below, D53 amended the same day): fed by a hearth they never reached, they died of hunger with no meal, in both pooled runs; in the control they gathered there and lived.
  - **What a body sees, at dawn, at its hearth** (the circle read at dawn, D55): the reserve, and the hunger of its own present (its spouse, its children under sixteen, at `HUNGRY` or more). One that sees its own hungry sets out to gather for them too, as many days of their need as it stores for itself, less what the reserve holds. Who lay down at no hearth saw nothing that morning.
  - **The care for the young's coming days** (« les jeunes d'abord »): a grown body counts in its own store its share of what the reserve leaves uncovered of the young of its hearth's next `care_days` (2) of need, each young one weighed by its care, its own children by twice it (`own_care_percent` 200, at most the whole). The care is innate, drawn even in `care_percent` (0 to 100) from the run's seed and its id (salt `HEARTH_CARE`), never inherited, as the will (D50). It sets out for **the greater** of the two, its own seen hungry or its care, never both: they weigh the same children. This is what keeps a grown body going out when the reserve is full for itself.
  - **Not built**: giving outside the hearth, debts, a sharer's reputation, meat (with the hunt).
  - **Content, not code** (D27, D30): `needs.ron`, section `pooling`: `fed_until_years` 8, `care_percent` (0, 100), `own_care_percent` 200, `care_days` 2, each explained there; absent, nothing is pooled and a weaned child gathers for itself. The vivarium's controls: `--no-pooling` (the section replaced by none) and `--no-care` (`care_days` 0).
  - **Measured**: the vivarium's JSON block `pooling` (`docs/10-vivarium-data.md`): what each hearth received, gave and lost day by day, the reserves at dawn and the share of the grown going out, the parents of a child under eight against the other grown (tiles walked, fatigue at nightfall, thin and weak days), the children dead before eight and before sixteen; the lab's run page reads it (« Le foyer »). The years' food balance (D55's `food`) counts what is put down at a hearth as leaving the packs and what is eaten there as eaten from no pack, by the putter's and the eater's tribes, so that its `lost_kj` stays what the dead left (two tribes of the vivarium's small tests share one hearth). **The food carried home** (measures only, the project lead, 25 November 2026, after half the tribe died of hunger at richness 1.0): every death of hunger with what the body carried for its hearth and of its own, its distance to its camp, its last meal, its age, will and children, its hearth's reserve then (`pooling.hunger_deaths`); the gathers ended with the arms full and what the plant still held (`pooling.arms`); who ate at the hearth (`pooling.eaters`); the grown's intake against their need by season (`pooling.intake`); the hunger gauge of the bodies holding food for their hearth, at each thought and each game hour (`pooling.carrying`). The world is the same with them as without (the JSON of seeds 1 to 5 identical but for these keys).
  - **Measured on 25 November 2026** (seed 1, richness 1.0, `--world vivarium-wide`, two tribes of 25, 144 days; control `--no-pooling`, then pooling without the care `--no-care`, then with it): births 28 → 19 → 19, of which conceived during the run 9 → 1 → 1; deaths 21 → 16 → 16, of hunger 4 → 1 → 1; alive at the end 57 → 53 → 53; children dead before eight 9 of 28 → 6 of 19 → 6 of 19; couples together at dawn 51 → 48 → 46 %. Put down 15.5 and 19.7 GJ, eaten 15.1 and 16.8 GJ (by the young 5.3 and 6.3), spoiled 0.44 and 0.22 GJ; the reserves at dawn 1.0 and 1.7 GJ on average. Tiles walked a day: the parents of a child under eight 4,419 → 4,622 → 4,520, the other grown 5,235 → 4,859 → 4,816; fatigue at nightfall 77 in all three. **No boom and bust**: the share of the grown gathering does not follow the reserve (r = 0.07 and −0.08); its days under half are the same days in the three runs, the seasons'. The fall in births: woman-dawns with the reserve to conceive and her spouse within reach 21 → 12; with pooling those women slept 120 to 320 tiles from their husbands (sleeping out apart 65 → 88 → 142 dawns); why is not yet known. One seed: to be confirmed on more.
  - **Nothing stored, no protocol change** (D28 unchanged): the reserves live in the brains, as the minds (D50, D55); the heaps are the world's items as before. No dependency.
  - **Amended on 25 November 2026 (the life lot) — the young first at twelve, in the content.** The project lead, approving the audit of life and population (`content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/audit-vie-population-2026-11-25.md`, proposal 1 and defects 1 and 8): "Non, c'est parfait, c'est même incroyable… C'est très très bien ce qu'ils ont suggéré. Je suis partant, on y va." The age under which the young of a hearth eat from its reserve before the others was sixteen, written in the code (`GROWN_MILLI`); in the lean third spring of the 25 November runs 82 of the 179 founders still alive died of hunger in sixteen days, at every age alike, while 6 children did: the twelve-to-sixteen-year-olds, who eat 65 to 90 % of a grown body, ate ahead of the parents. It is now content: `pooling.young_first_until_years`, **12** (the Hadza and !Kung children of ten to twelve gather much of their own food), validated at least `fed_until_years` and at most 100; absent, sixteen as before, so older content reads the same. From twelve a child eats at the hearth as the grown do, only what the reserve holds beyond the younger ones' appetite of the day.
    - **Decided alone, told:** (1) the care for the young's coming days follows the same age: the young cared for are those under twelve, and a body from twelve counts their uncovered days in its store as the grown do (before, only from sixteen); (2) `is_own`, whose hunger a parent sees at dawn (its spouse, its children under sixteen), stays at sixteen, a constant of the code (`OWN_CHILD_MILLI`): it is the family's sight, not the hearth's order; (3) the vivarium's measures keep sixteen (the young class of `carried`, the `pooling` block's `eight_to_sixteen_kj` and `dead_before_sixteen`), so runs before and after compare; (4) the field is a D30 placeholder, as the section's others.
  - **Amended on 25 November 2026 (lightening) — the hearth's reserve as a ledger.** The project lead, approving technique 2 of the third lightening audit (`content/ai-reserve/review/allegement-mesures/audit-allegement-3-2026-11-25.md`): "c'est simplement un bookkeeping. Donc les objets disparaissent du jeu, elles sont simplement dans la réserve mais notées, en mémoire, et quand quelqu'un en prend, hop, elle est créée… quelqu'un vient manger, elle est enlevée de la réserve, et le statut de la personne, lui, est modifié." Reason: every look of every body read, one by one, all the items lying in the chunks in sight, the hearth's heaps among them, to learn that they were not food lying about; the audit measured these reads at 15 % of a run at 25 per tribe and 67–69 % at 50 per tribe mid-run, growing faster than the population. Counted before the change (seed 1, `--world vivarium-wide`, two tribes): up to **1,263 heaps** in the reserves at a dawn at 25 per tribe (160 days) and **3,600** at 50 per tribe (64 days).
    - **What changes.** Putting down at the reserve is a new intention of the world, `Intention::Keep` (refused as a drop is): the heap leaves the world's items and the ground for a **record** the world keeps off the ground (`World::kept`: its material, grams, the tick it was made, where it is kept; `Event::Kept`), which no look and no chunk holds. **Eating** from it is the world's eating gesture (`Intention::Eat`, unchanged, the same reach, spoilage and meal): the body is fed, the record is lighter (`Event::Kept` again) or gone, **no item is made**. **Taken away** (a hand's trial may take what lies near, and did: 5,240 times in 160 days at 25 per tribe), the record **is an item again**, the same id, mass and date, carried. It **spoils** in the store as anywhere (`Event::Spoiled`, no holder). The hearth's ledger (which camp, its tile, its records in order) stays the brain's (`Reserves`), read by ids, never by a scan; the dawn's sight, the young first, the hunger bar, the care and the measures read it as before.
    - **Behaviour unchanged by design**: the eating, the order of the heaps, the spoilage tick, the reach, the hands' sight of what lies near (the records are among the things near) and their taking are the world's same rules; the pages say a keep as the drop they said before. **Not verified on runs**: the lead stopped the proof runs (25 November 2026: proofs later, only if there is a doubt); the unit tests cover the store (in, eaten whole and in part, spoiled, taken away, deterministic) and the pooling tests pass unchanged but for the intention's name. The gain is not measured.
    - **Nothing stored, no protocol change** (D28 unchanged): the records are never persisted, as the minds are not (D50); only the vivarium's brains keep food, the server's worlds hold none and the server ignores `Event::Kept`. No dependency.
    - **Decided alone, told:** (1) the records are held by the world, not the brain, because the world alone feeds a body and applies gestures in order (two bodies eating one heap in a tick); (2) a record keeps the item's shape and id, so that a heap taken away is the same item again and the measures that follow an item by id are unchanged; (3) a heap of a reserve taken away and let fall there again as an item (its carrier's death) still counts in the reserve, as before; (4) the pages print a keep as `Drop { … }`, so that runs before and after compare; (5) found and not fixed (a change of behaviour, the lead's call): a heap taken away by the hands and laid again before the next dawn is listed twice in its reserve until that dawn, and counted twice; the stored items lying elsewhere (branches, pebbles, birch bark, flint, corpses: 1,624 at 160 days at 25 per tribe, 532 at 64 days at 50 per tribe, about ten more a day) grow slowly and were left as they are.
  - **Amended on 25 November 2026 — the store counts what is laid at home.** Approved by the project lead on 25 November 2026, from the growth study (`content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/etude-croissance-2026-11-25.md`, §3a: « la provision d'un corps compte aussi ce qu'il a posé chez lui »). **The measured reason** (four seeds): 135 of 149 hunger deaths (91 %) fell in spring, 121 of them in its second half; autumn was 97–99 % wasted (380 to 650 GJ on the plants within reach of the two camps, 5 to 11 GJ gathered) while the hearth never held more than 1.2 GJ. The cause was the rule: a body's store (the D50 way `stock_kj`, 30 MJ) was **what it carried**, and arms hold 1 kg of food (11.5 MJ of hazelnuts), so the store was never reached and the trial « stock ×2 » (60 MJ) could never succeed: it was never kept (median 30 MJ in every seed and cycle).
    - **What changes.** A body's store is measured where a forager keeps it: **what it carries plus its share of its hearth's reserve**, the reserve's kilojoules divided by those who lay down in its circle at dawn (`Reserves::share_kj`; nothing without pooling or before a dawn saw that circle). It gathers until the two reach its stock way and what it owes its hearth (as before, with the share added), and at the fire's morning plan it stays when they cover its stock and a day's need. **At its camp, its store short, it keeps nothing on itself and lays all it carries down** (a heap a thought), its arms free to fetch the rest; its store reached, it keeps at most its stock on itself and lays the rest, as before. « Stock ×2 » now means more trips with full arms to fill the hearth, judged by the world over the trial's three seasons (#367). No feeling, goal or bonus is added: the same way, measured at home.
    - **The share, not the pile.** Each eater counts only its share, so one pile does not stop the whole hearth from gathering: four eaters around a pile worth one store each see a quarter of it. A body whose share covers its store rests; as the hearth eats the reserve down, the shares fall and they gather again.
    - **Decided alone, told:** (1) the divisor is everyone who lay down in the circle at dawn, the young included (they eat from it), read once a dawn; (2) a short store empties the arms at the camp: the brief said what is kept on oneself stays capped as before, but with arms smaller than any store nothing would ever be laid and « stock ×2 » would stay impossible, so short of its store a body keeps nothing on itself (it eats from the reserve first anyway, D58); (3) a child fed by its hearth keeps the old rule; (4) the reserve's kilojoules are read at most once a tick per hearth (a reading forgotten when a heap is put down), to keep the cost of the share out of every thought.
    - **Not verified on runs** (the lead chooses them). No protocol, storage or dependency change.
  - **Amended on 26 November 2026 (lightening) — the reserve's records grouped into heaps.** The project lead, 26 November 2026, from the fourth lightening audit (`content/ai-reserve/review/allegement-mesures/audit-allegement-4-2026-11-26.md`, reductions 1 and 10): « regrouper les lignes de la réserve en tas par type de nourriture. C'est simplement de la comptabilité. Il faut tout regrouper : 125 noisettes, ce n'est pas 125 fois une noisette mais une fois 125 noisettes. Et les regrouper par jour : avec 1000 noisettes, 250 d'hier, 300 d'aujourd'hui, le reste des jours d'avant. » **The measured reason**: each food laid at a hearth stayed a record of its own, up to 16,730 in one reserve, and every look at the reserve (what it holds, the heap that spoils first, a body's share) read them all, 6 to 20 million records a journée: the reserve was 17 to 24 % of a run.
    - **What changes.** (1) **Heaps, in the world** (`World::keep`): food laid in a store **joins the record kept at that very tile of the same food whose heap goes spoiled at the start of the same journée** (the key: tile, material, and that journée, `World::heap_goes_on`: the journée whose start would have taken the item away loose, `(made + keeping).div_ceil(day)`, brought down to the first of its band of `pooling.heap_days` journées, 1 in `needs.ron`); dated as the older of the two (`Item::merge`); the item laid is no more, and `Event::Kept` says which record took it in (`into`). What never spoils nor wears is never joined. (2) **A heap spoils by the journée** (the lead, 26 November 2026): a record kept in a store is good until the start of its heap's journée and goes whole then (`World::spoils_at`); with `heap_days` 1 every part goes at the very journée start it went at loose, and none spoils earlier inside its last journée. (3) **A heap knows its count** (« une fois 125 noisettes »): the world counts the items a record was laid as (`kept_parts`, derived, never persisted), and **a bite from a heap is one of them at most** (their mean, rounded up), as a bite from the item laid was; a whole one eaten lowers the count. (4) **Three acts, named apart** (the lead, 26 November 2026): **pick up** (`Gesture::PickUp`, « take » before, « pick up » in the vivarium's names): the hands pick up only what lies in the world in no hearth's reserve; the heaps of any hearth, its own or another's, are never picked up (`World::items_near` no longer lists them; taking another camp's store comes much later, with rights). **Take from the common reserve**: the hearth's own act, eating there (the young first, the hunger bar), unchanged. **Take at home**, a family's own stash, is a third act, distinct, not built: it comes later with rights to the common reserve (« not everyone will be able to take from it »). Gathering from plants stays its own act. The world's pick-up of a kept record, which no brain asks for now, takes a handful: what the arms and load allow, the rest staying kept (`Event::Kept` from the heap to the rest). (5) **The reserve's reading kept** (`Reserves`): a reserve's heaps are read once after each tick in which the world's records changed (`World::kept_changes`), in the order they spoil with their kilojoules in all; a look leaves out the first heaps whose time has come meanwhile. This replaces the reading kept once a tick per hearth (decided alone (4) of the amendment above, the store counts what is laid at home). (6) **A heap counts once**: the reserve's records are a set, so a heap taken away and laid again before dawn is no longer counted twice (defect 22 of `content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/liste-defauts-v2-2026-11-25.md`).
    - **What may differ, and why**: (a) kept food stays good up to the end of its last journée (it was refused from its very tick on, and removed only at the next journée's start anyway); (b) the hands no longer pick up from a reserve (they took 7k to 61k items a tribe in 192 journées, most of them laid back at once); (c) a heap taken away and let fall there again (its carrier's death) is no longer the reserve's (decided alone (3) of the ledger amendment): the reading follows the world's records alone; (d) a bite is the mean of a heap's items, not one item's own weight. The order of eating is unchanged: what spoils first first, then by id. `heap_days` 3 would make fewer heaps, but a heap goes at its band's first journée: berries, which keep three journées, would lose up to two.
    - **Measured** (seeds 5, 6 and 7, `--world vivarium-wide`, two tribes of 25, 192 journées; before: main with #379 and #380, after: this change on it, run side by side; tribes A then B). Records in a reserve at dawn, most and mean: 3,913 / 557 and 1,660 / 235 → 39 / 11 and 32 / 7.5 (seed 6: 4,750 / 360 and 5,766 / 170 → 8 / 2.6 and 36 / 8.1; seed 7: 5,245 / 722 and 3,106 / 152 → 29 / 11 and 27 / 6.9). **Wall time 506 → 329 s (−35 %), 461 → 380 s (−18 %), 339 → 231 s (−32 %).** Alive at the end 44, 38 → 34, 38 (seed 6: 36, 37 → 35, 40; seed 7: 32, 32 → 38, 36); births 41, 32 → 28, 34 (34, 34 → 36, 33; 29, 26 → 33, 33): **196 → 197 over the three seeds**; no death of hunger. Eaten from the reserves by each tribe's people: 28.2, 22.0 → 23.1, 24.2 GJ (27.6, 23.5 → 32.5, 24.2; 17.8, 21.8 → 28.6, 23.8); bites of a mean 46–68 g → 56–73 g. Picked up from a reserve by the hands: 60.6k, 7.2k → 0 (14.6k, 12.0k → 0; 27.1k, 17.1k → 0). Spoiled at the hearths: 31 → 61 MJ, 23 → 239 MJ, 205 → 36 MJ (the lead: some rot at the hearth is natural, « en famine rien ne pourrit »).
    - **Why the hands no longer pick up from a store** (counted, not supposed): a body that knew the deed « take » of a food (D57) and lacked it took it from its hearth's reserve; short of its store at its camp, it laid all it carried down at once (the amendment above), lacked it again and took it again: **one take every two ticks**. Before heaps, one body took the same 11 g item **680 times in a row**; with heaps taken by the handful, **34k to 535k takes a tribe** in 192 journées, and the run grew slower than before heaps.
    - **Nothing stored, no protocol change** (D28 unchanged): the records and their counts are never persisted; `Event::Kept` gains a field the server ignores; the player's `Take` intent of the protocol is another thing, untouched. No dependency.
  - **Amended on 26 November 2026 (lightening) — food laid at the hearth wakes nobody.** The project lead, 26 November 2026, from the study of thoughts (`content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/etude-penser-moins-2026-11-26.md`, §3.3 and §8, step A only), first « un dormeur ne voit pas le foyer », then sharpened the same day: food laid at or taken from the hearth's reserve wakes **nobody, awake or asleep** — "in real life it doesn't happen like that, and it serves nothing. People must go and look at the hearth when they are hungry." **The measured reason**: 48 % of all thoughts came from food laid at or taken from a reserve, which made every body of that hearth at the camp think again, sleepers included (44 % of those wakes); 90 % of those thoughts changed nothing.
    - **What changes.** `Event::Kept` and `Event::ItemAdded` of a reserve's food no longer make anyone due (`Brains::hearth_events`). A hungry body thinks by its own wakes, as everywhere: a gauge past a threshold, a gather or gesture ended, arrived or stopped, refused, dawn and dusk, a group's wait, its point of no return; then, at its camp, it eats from the reserve as before (D58 above). Nothing else in how people think changes: the routine of the day (the study's B) is refused ("we don't want robots") and copying a day's place (C) is for later. What a body knows of its reserve from afar (`Reserves::share_kj`) is untouched.
    - **What it costs, said**: a hungry body already waiting idle at its camp (a child under eight fed by its hearth, a grown one bearing its hunger out of season) no longer eats the moment food is laid there, but at its own next wake: dusk or dawn at the latest, sooner if a gauge or an event comes (a test shows both rules). The wake was added in the first trial of pooling, where a hungry child waiting at the camp starved beside a full reserve; dawn and dusk wake every mind, so the wait is bounded, and the runs will say whether the young suffer from it.
    - **Content, not code** (D27, D30): `needs.ron`, section `thought`, `hearth_wakes: false` (absent: false); `true` is the rule before. The vivarium's control: `--wake-all` (sets it true).
    - **Measured** (seed 17, `--world vivarium-wide`, two tribes of 25, 64 journées, main and the new rule run side by side). With `--wake-all` the JSON without `meta.timing` and the new factor `thought.hearth_wakes` is **bit-identical to main** (`9bb04c664ec70ebb…`). The new rule (`725a14eb08e4c00a…`): thoughts **1,114,088 → 582,407 (−48 %)**; the run's wall time 107.2 → 100.8 s (−6 %, within the ±20 % noise of runs side by side); alive at the end 67 → 64, births 26 → 24, deaths 9 → 10 (illness 7 → 8, old age 2 and 2), hunger deaths 0 and 0, children dead before eight 5 → 6; eaten at the hearths 12.98 → 11.86 MJ (the young 4.77 → 4.81 MJ, under eight 3.10 → 2.89 MJ); hours weak 130 → 49, hours thin 13,958 → 13,055. One seed: the population's differences are a new course of the same world, not yet a trend.
    - **Nothing stored, no protocol change** (D28 unchanged). No dependency.
  - **Amended on 26 November 2026 (the audit of wakes, step 4) — the remembered look at the hearth.** The project lead, 26 November 2026: "No telepathy. A person knows what is at the camp when she sees it. If she is out and believes there is a big reserve but it has been emptied, she will know only when she comes back. If she believes there is nothing but many came home to fill it, she will know it too by looking." The audit `content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/audit-reveils-2026-11-26.md`, §3.1.
    - **What changes.** Each mind keeps what it saw of its hearth's reserve the last time it saw it, and when (`HearthSeen`: the kilojoules the reserve held, how many lay down in its circle at dawn, the tick). It is taken at every thought where the body is awake at its camp (`at_camp`: within 6 tiles of its home, or on its home's place; not its whole sight, one does not count heaps of berries from 60 tiles: a choice of the audit's), at the dawn circle (`see_hearths`, those who lay there), and grows by what it lays there itself. Its share, wherever the store is weighed (« am I full? » in `decide`, `store_short`, the fire's « it stays »), is what it saw divided by the circle it saw, believed as seen: nothing projected (spoiled, eaten, laid since). Eating at the reserve (`eat_at_hearth`) stands before the heaps and keeps the true state. Measured in the audit: away from the camp the answer « am I full? » changes in 0.9 % of its reads (0.18 a person-journée).
    - **Content** (D27, D30): `needs.ron`, section `pooling`, `hearth_look: true`; `false`, or absent, is the rule before, `Reserves::share_kj` read from anywhere (`--old-wakes`).
    - Nothing stored (a mind lives in memory), **no protocol change, no storage change, no dependency**.
  - **Amended on 26 November 2026 (the audit of wakes, step 5) — the young are seen, not their stomach.** The project lead's own point, 26 November 2026, from the case of PR #386: a hungry child waiting at the camp no longer eats when food is laid there, since that wakes nobody; « no telepathy » and « we don't want robots ». The audit `content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/audit-reveils-2026-11-26.md`, §3.2 A.
    - **Feeding the young** (`pooling::feed_young`, before a body puts its food down). An awake body grown, or a child past `fed_until_years`, who sees a young one of its hearth hungry near it (one its circle saw at dawn, fed by the hearth, not a nursed infant, awake, hunger at 50 or more, within 6 tiles, the nearest) feeds it: what it carries, walking to it first; or, at its camp with nothing to give, a handful taken from the reserve's heap that spoils first, the hearth's food, laid back once the young is fed. A perception of the giver, never a wake of the young: the young eats what it is handed and thinks nothing of it (its meal makes it due no more than its own order's echo). A new act of the world, `Intention::Feed { body, young, item }` (the young on the giver's tile or next to it, the item within the giver's reach; eaten as by `Eat`, to the young's own room; refused as an eating, told to the giver); a gesture of the simulation's own: no client asks it, **no protocol message**, never stored (pending intentions are not persisted).
    - **A mouthful after another** (fixed the same day, counted on seed 21): the giver looks again at once after each mouthful it gives, gives another while the young is still hungry, then lays the rest back. Without it, nothing told the giver its mouthful was eaten: it stood idle a mouthful later, a handful from the reserve in its arms (587 handfuls taken, 0.96 M kJ given by hand, the young under eight at hunger 100 for 7,040 hours against 5,150 under the rule before). A young one with no room left (it refuses food) is not one to feed.
    - **The hunger bar holds for the giver** (fixed the same day, counted on seed 21): 69 % of the mouthfuls given came from a giver itself hungry, most at hunger 100. A giver whose own hunger gives way (`gives_way`: very hungry and not kept by its will, or starving) eats first and gives nothing, as the lead's bar says of the reserve: « avoir très très faim, mourir de faim et avoir de la nourriture, ça personne ne le fera jamais ».
    - **The giver's care** (amended 26 November 2026, the project lead: « an old man who goes without for his grandchildren is a story; an old man who ruins himself for any child of the camp is a defect »). A body may go without food for the young, but only through the innate care it already has (`care_percent`, drawn for life), weighing more for its own. A giver that is short, hungry or under `giver_short_fat_percent` (50) of its fat reserve, feeds a young one of its hearth only on the journées its care draws it to: its care for any young, its care for its own (`own_care_percent`, at most certain) for its child or grandchild; under `giver_kin_only_fat_percent` (25) of its fat, its own only. The draw is made once a journée for each giver and young (`purpose::GIVER_CARE`), so it does not change its mind each thought; a young one it is not drawn to is not seen as one to feed, and the next is. The hunger bar no longer decides for the giver under this rule, but **a starving giver gives nothing, even to its own**: under `giver_starving_fat_percent` (15) of its fat it eats first (the project lead, 26 November 2026, his rule of 25 November kept: « mourir de faim et avoir de la nourriture, ça personne ne le fera jamais, on mange la nourriture », the floor lowered from 25 to 15 for the giver: « à la limite on peut peut-être le descendre à 15 % »). From 15 to 25 % it feeds its own only, from 25 to 50 % as its care says. A grandchild is read through the young's parents still in the world: a dead parent's line is not read, and the bodies placed at the world's birth have no parents, so their kin is not known as such. Why: on seed 22 the two weak elders (about 62 and 75) gave 67 and 66 mouthfuls, all to children not their own; their weakness came mostly from the lean first month and their age (both already weak under the rule before, fat down to 12–14 %), but gifts from hungry givers to any child were the defect the lead named. `giver_care: false`, or absent, is the hunger bar for the giver, the rule above.
    - **What the reserve keeps for the young** (`young_ahead`, the « young first » of a hungry body eating at the reserve) counts the stomach (`room_j`) of the young it sees at its camp only; for each other young its circle saw at dawn, a day of its need less what it was seen eating at its camp since dawn. The audit: 11.8 such reads a person-journée, 63 % with a young one away from the camp.
    - **Content** (D27, D30): `needs.ron`, section `pooling`, `young_seen: true`; `false`, or absent, is the rule before (`--old-wakes`). `giver_care: true`, `giver_short_fat_percent: 50`, `giver_kin_only_fat_percent: 25`, `giver_starving_fat_percent: 15`; `giver_care: false`, or absent, is the hunger bar for the giver (`--old-wakes` sets it false too).
    - Nothing stored, **no protocol change, no storage change, no dependency**.

### D59 — Learning causes: believe without acting (first step)
- **Reason**: the project lead, 26 November 2026, named it the key question: how does a body notice that an outcome repeats (a fouled camp, then the sick) and come to a cause of its own, with no feeling engineered toward the answer and no hidden number read? The study `content/ai-reserve/review/vivarium/lot-5/etude-apprentissage-2026-11-27.md` proposed a chain on the pieces that already work (the problems in mind, D50 amended 26 November; the well and the fire, D55, D56): a mark leaves an **episode**, the one who **notices** **cross-checks** its episodes, what was always there becomes a **belief**, told and copied; later its kind gives a lever, the world judges the trial. The lead approved the study and its five recommendations on 27 November 2026 (the judge by the problem as perceived; no visible dirt yet, the nights slept at the camp stand for it; two steps, believing first; the will as the depth of the search; the tradition). This decision is the **first step only: believe, without acting.** Why first: "tiny first" — one run shows who believes what, true or false, and who the surprising people are, before any behaviour changes.
- **Consequences**:
  - **A mark is lived, never added.** Falling ill (`Event::FellIll`, D49 amended 26 November 2026), and, at the dawn's fire, seeing a kin sick (spouse, child under sixteen, parent: the family #342 knows). The mark pushes the ailment's problem (`ill_gut` or `ill_chest`, two kinds, because a body feels the gut from the chest) on the body's things to think about (D50, amended 27 November). A stranger seen sick marks nobody: it is only noted as "someone sick at my fire".
  - **The episode** (`memory::Record::Episode`, D55): the sick writes it once, at most one of each circumstance the content names, each perceived by the body itself: its camp (the place of its fire at dusk), the nights it slept at that fire in a row (new, settled, old: **the stand-in for filth**), the season, how many lay at its fire last night (a few, some, many), a cold night, someone sick at its fire these two nights, what it ate most the day before, where it last drank. A kin who sees it takes an access to the sick's episode, as the well wants; a mate at the fire who already carries the problem takes one too, without a mark. A problem keeps its last `episodes_kept` (8); the queue is let go when the problem fades from the list.
  - **Noticing is a draw at a mark**, never a scan: with at least `notice_from` (3) episodes, by the will (`notice_percent_by_will`, about five times likelier for the willing; 1 % at a will of 0, so anyone can), in proportion to the episodes kept, and twice for a child of its own seen sick (`child_times`).
  - **Cross-checking** lays the latest episodes side by side, as many as the will looks at (`depth_by_will`: 3 for the timid, 8 for the willing — not a level, a number of memories): what was there each time (all but once with `all_but_one_from`, 6, or more) is suspect; at most `suspects_max` (2), drawn among more. Nothing there: it noticed, and "sees nothing". Few episodes let a coincidence through, so the timid are wrong more often: false beliefs are born of the mechanism itself, never coded.
  - **The belief** (`memory::Record::Belief`): the problem, its suspects, each with the **kind** of cause the content gives it (`place`, `season`, `weather`, `company`, `food`, `water`, `gesture`). The kind is recorded and read by nothing yet: it will give the lever in the second step. One belief a problem a body; its own new one replaces the old.
  - **Told at the fire, copied, kept by tradition.** At the dawn's fire a body tells its fresh beliefs (just come to, just taken, or renewed by a mark), at most `told_per_night` (2), apart from the fire's other slots. A listener takes one when it carries the same problem, or is a child under `child_years` (16) — **the tradition**: kept without having lived it — and its credit in the teller (D55's function) reaches `copy_credit` (50), halved past the belief's whole seasons; it never gives up its own, and replaces a heard one only for one believed more. The same belief heard renews it, and is not told again for that: no tale loops round a fire.
  - **Kept or forgotten.** A belief counts whole `whole_seasons` (a cycle) from its last renewal (a mark whose episode holds all its suspects, or the same belief heard), at half `half_seasons` more, then it is forgotten. An episode without its suspects counters it; `counter_examples` (3) make it fall. Everything is bounded by body (3 problems × 8 episodes, a belief a problem) and fades: nothing grows with time.
  - **No action.** No lever, no move, no trial comes of a belief in this step; the problems felt begin no trial of the hands (`trials_per_season: 0`).
  - **No hidden number.** The brain never reads a place's fouling, the odds of falling ill, or any state the body does not perceive; being sick, and of what, is perceived (a body feels its gut; one sees the sick at the fire). Two tests prove it: two worlds alike but for their camps' fouling give the same episodes, beliefs and minds; and no line of the brain's code names the illness rule's hidden numbers. Only the vivarium reads the rule, to classify each belief true, partly or false in its report: a measure, not a perception.
  - **The control** (`--no-learning` in the vivarium: the content's `learning` section replaced by none and the problems it marks taken out): behaviour is bit-identical to the code before this change. Checked on one run, seed 25, 64 journées, vivarium-wide, two tribes of 25: main and this branch with the flag give the same data (sha256 of the JSON without `meta.timing` and the new keys).
  - **Measured** (`docs/10`, block `learning`, and `meta.timing.learning_s`): marks lived (own, kin), noticing by band of will (timid under 20, willing from 50), beliefs born by content and verdict, told, copied and by tradition, renewed, countered and forgotten, the beliefs held each season (own, heard, by tradition, by children), and the wall time the learning takes.
  - **Content** (D27, D30): `needs.ron`, section `learning`, and the kinds `ill_gut`, `ill_chest` of `problems`; every value a placeholder, public (not a hidden rule of D30). Absent: nothing is learnt.
  - **Decided alone, told**: (1) the nights at a camp count the dawns a body lay at its fire; a night slept out leaves the count as it was, a night at another fire starts it again; (2) a sick infant is seen in its carrier's arms though it is not in the fire's circle: its mother, and its father at the same fire, see their own child sick and weigh it `child_times` (the project lead, approving #390 on 27 November 2026); (3) "what it ate most" is the day before's, by grams eaten; "where it drank" is the place of its last drink; (4) a belief told is apart from the fire's six slots (`told_per_night` of its own), and a copied belief is told once by its new holder; (5) the credit of a heard belief is kept to weigh a later one; (6) the camp and the place drunk at count as "partly true" in the vivarium's verdict (where the fouling is, but staying long is the cause), many at the fire "partly" for the gut, some "partly" for the chest.
  - **No protocol change, no storage change, no dependency.** The heads live in the vivarium only (D50): no snapshot holds them.
- **Amended 27 November 2026, second step: act** (the project lead approved the second step that day, then answered its open question, Q1 = B extended with fear and reward, and Q2). Why: the beliefs exist; now a belief of a place makes its holders leave, and the world judges whether leaving helped.
  - **A sign.** Every belief carries one (`memory::Sign`): a **fear** pushes away from what it accuses, a **reward** pulls toward it. Illness beliefs are fears. Nothing gives a reward yet; the sign is recorded so that one can.
  - **A strength, from what was lived.** Its gravest episode gives it: a kin's death of it (`strength_kin_death`, 100) above one's own illness (`strength_own`, 60) above a sick body seen at the fire (`strength_seen`, 30). A belief heard carries `heard_percent` (50) of its teller's strength now. It is whole for the belief's whole seasons from its last renewal, then half: it fades unless renewed (a mark that agrees, the same heard, a trial kept), each renewal keeping the greater of the two. A kin who held the episode of one who died of it holds it at its gravest, and its belief that the episode agrees with rises.
  - **The lever comes from the kind of its suspects** (`acting.levers`): a place means **leave**, the only lever built. The others (`prepare` for a season or the weather, `keep_apart` for company, `avoid` for food, `drink_elsewhere` for water, `otherwise` for a gesture) are only recorded where they would apply (`learning.acting.would`).
  - **Meaning to leave.** A mark whose episode holds every suspect of the belief its holder has of that problem: of a place, its will draws whether it means to act, at the chance a distressed body tries (`trials.try_percent_by_will`, D50). At its next free nightfall (no trial, no call, grown), after the moves called as before, it calls a move (D56) to the best place its own notes know (lived a day there at least), not its home nor a camp its belief accuses, no worse for food by those notes than `worse_permille` (100, a tenth of a day of need, Q2), net of the move's cost; knowing none, it waits for another mark. Its fire weighs the call as any other.
  - **The weight on moves (Q1 = B).** Every holder of a belief of a place, its own, heard or by tradition, adds to its own balance on any move it weighs at the fire (and to whether the place is worth it): strength × `fear_tenths` / 10 (5 a point: 300 for one's own illness) per mille of a day of need for leaving here when here is as the belief accuses, the same against going to a camp it names; a reward the other way, at `reward_tenths` (2.5 a point: fear weighs twice). Everyone else decides as before, and family still pulls. Nothing changes the choice of the place a move is called to by its notes (`lived_best`), nor the judgment of a home trial by the place's grade.
  - **The judge is the problem as perceived**: the ill episodes its body saw, each once, its own ills, its kin's, and the sick at its fire; never the sim's count. A move home away begins a trial of a season: from a belief, when its call made it; **by chance**, when the move was for another reason (or followed someone's call) and it carries an ill problem. At its end, with `judge_nights` (8) dawns away from the camp left and ills perceived the season before: **kept** when those of the season after are at most `keep_percent` (50) of them, its belief renewed and told as a lesson at the next fire (by chance, a belief of the camp left and its age, its strength from its gravest episode); else **abandoned**, its belief countered (a step to its fall). Otherwise unjudged. A new move ends a trial unjudged.
  - **The horizon, not built: the taboo.** A place feared and told down the generations should become a dreaded place ("a cursed forest"). Nothing blocks it: a fear of a place taken by tradition weighs on those who never lived it, against going there, as on its first holder; what is still missing is a fear kept beyond its seasons (it fades today) and a place feared by many.
  - **Controls.** `--no-learning` stays bit-identical to the code before the change: checked again on one run, seed 25, 64 journées, vivarium-wide, two tribes of 25 (sha256 of the JSON without `meta.timing` and the new keys). `--learning-believe-only` plays the first step alone (the content's `learning.acting` replaced by none): beliefs and their tales, no lever, no weight, no trial; its moves are still counted.
  - **Measured** (`docs/10`, `learning.acting`, and `meta.timing.learning_s`): who meant to leave, the levers that would apply, the calls, waits and calls dropped, the trials from a belief and by chance with their ends, the lessons told, the moves by their cause and the nights at the camp left (its age at leaving), and season by season the gut ills per living day.
  - **Content**: `needs.ron`, `learning.acting`, every value a placeholder with its comment.
  - **Decided alone, told**: (1) the will's chance to mean to leave is the distressed one (a mark is an illness lived); (2) a place known means a day lived there by its own notes; (3) a chance trial takes the first ill problem the mover carries; (4) one trial at a time, a new move ending the last unjudged; (5) "away" counts the dawns woken at a fire in another place; (6) a chance trial kept writes its belief without a draw (finding the ills gone is the noticing); (7) a follower of a belief's call counts as a move from a belief, and its trial is by chance; (8) without acting the moves are still counted, for the comparison.
  - **No protocol change, no storage change, no dependency.**
